## Working mode (owner instruction, `docs/step2/02_Owner_Decisions_Step2.md`)
Run steps 3, 4, 7, 8, 9 without stopping. **Stop only for:** step 5 design directions, step 6 mockups (no UI code before approval), chart-of-accounts template approval (D-10), real blockers (all three approvals given by 10 Oct 2026; only real blockers remain). New ambiguities: choose the recommended option, record it under "Decisions taken without asking", continue. Quality gates per module (build, all tests green, code review, security review for auth/isolation/money). After each module: update this file and commit locally (never push).

- **Owner feedback of 13 Oct 2026, second round — report builder error (SCR-52), one commit, not pushed:**
  - **Cause:** not detail mode, not a row limit. A new summary report opened with *no* row grouping: under the "Row grouping" heading there was only the "+ Grouping" button, and the first list below it was the *column* grouping. "Trip date by month" chosen there sent a summary with zero row groupings, which the server refuses (1–2 row groupings needed) with the generic range message naming the internal path `definition.rows`. When detail mode is really on, the screen sends only the columns and the server ignores groupings and measures, so that combination never fails.
  - **Screen:** summary mode starts with one empty "Grouping 1"; the column grouping has its own heading and an "optional" hint; in detail mode groupings and measures stay hidden with a hint that they are kept and unused (DT-180). Show / Excel / PDF / background / save check the settings first (row grouping, a field for every measure but Count, 1–30 detail columns, filter values, period order) and mark the setting; nothing is sent. A refused run dims the earlier result under "The result below is not up to date" (not clickable, hidden from screen readers).
  - **Messages:** every refusal of a definition and of a saved report now has its own bilingual code (`report.*` in `ErrorCatalog`) that names the setting as the panel does ("Grouping 1", "Measure 2", "Filter 1", "Columns", "Top N") and says the limit and what to change; the field path stays only so the screen can mark the setting (`ReportProblems`, which also replaces any generic message as a safety net). Filter errors keep the filter's index as sent, also when the period replaces a trip-date filter.
  - **Banner:** the message element takes the banner's full width and wraps inside it, icon at the first line (all banners). Field error lines keep their text in one element, so a number inside an Arabic message no longer splits it into columns (found by the visual check; all fields).

Database changes allowed for these items only → **database design revision 6** (below). Testing rule: no E2E; tests of the touched code + build + filtered visual check (EN/AR, 1366/1920, `timeout 660 … --global-timeout=600000`).

2. **2FA optional at sign-up + "Require 2FA for all users" + security review — done (17 Oct 2026, DT-191, DT-192).** Migration `Rev6_RequireTwoFactor` (`core.Tenants.RequireTwoFactor`). `AuthService.SecondStepAsync` / `RequiresTwoFactorAsync` (staff always; tenant setting), `SignInNextStep.TwoFactorOffer` (first sign-in of an email user: stage `offer`, set-up page with "Skip for now" + recommendation, `POST /auth/2fa/skip`, audited `TwoFactorSkipped`), `CompleteWithoutSecondFactorAsync` (also records the sign-in and clears the failure counter for sign-ins without a code); tenant security tab toggle (Tenant → Companies → Security); turning it on ends sessions of users without 2FA. ## Owner decisions of 17 Oct 2026 on part A — progress (one commit each)
- **(4) + (6) Sign-in — done.** An email entered by an administrator gets a verification link (`UserAdminService.CreateWithPasswordAsync` → `AccountService.SendVerificationAsync`); `RequestPasswordResetAsync` sends nothing to an unverified email (platform staff excepted); the administrator's "send reset link" is refused with `user.emailNotVerified` (set a new password instead); sign-in does not wait for the verification. `AuthService.SecondStepAsync`: users with the preset Owner or Administrator role get `TwoFactorOffer` at every sign-in until 2FA is set up (skip allowed); others as before. Tests: `UserNameSignInTests` (+1, admin test skips the offer), `TwoFactorPolicyTests` (+1, Owner offer repeats).
- **(3) "Supplier deductions" not mapped — done.** The key stays optional; the bill form turns `apar.deductionKeyMissing` into an error banner with "Open account mapping" (settings.manage) or "ask a settings administrator". Adding it to the accounting reminders is listed under Open issues (owner: "later"). Test: `UI.Tests/AccountingPageTests` (+1).
- **(1) Returns give back the deduction — done.** Migration `Rev6_ReturnDeductions` (`acc.ApArReturns.Deduction`). A return's deduction = bill deduction × returned amount ÷ subtotal (the last return takes the rest), Total = Amount + Tax − Deduction, so a full return cancels the whole bill; the limit is the remaining payable plus the returned deduction share; entry Dr supplier (net) + Dr "Supplier deductions" (share), Cr lines and input tax; void restores the net amount. Shown in the return dialog (estimate), returns grid, return view, print and export. Test: `ApArTests` (+1).
- **(2) Supplier statement — done.** Standard report **RPT-24 "Supplier statement"** (`StandardReportService.SupplierStatementAsync`, parameter `supplier` = an accounting party with the Supplier role, required; acc.acct.apar or reports.view): opening balance owed, then bills (amount, tax, deduction, net = payable), purchase returns (the same, negative) and payments (net), running balance owed, totals; Excel/CSV/PDF through the report exporter; rows open the bill, return or payment. ## Owner decisions of 18 Oct 2026 on the security pass — done (DT-201 and DT-202 accepted the same day)
Testing rule of this round (owner): API security tests for each point, the 500,000-trip search timing, sign-in screen tests, build, visual check of sign-in / 2FA / change-password only (EN/AR, 1366/1920); no full E2E.
1. **Email verification link (DT-203) — done.** An email entered by an administrator (user created with a username) is confirmed only while that user is signed in: anonymous or another user's click → `auth.verifySignIn` (403); the page shows "sign in with that account's username" and a "Sign in to confirm" button that returns to the link after sign-in. The email says to sign in first. Until confirmed, no reset email goes to the address (unchanged). Self-registered and invited users (who sign in with that email) keep the link that works before sign-in. The signed-in confirmation no longer ends the user's sessions.
2. **"Already used" (DT-203) — done.** One generic message `user.notAvailable` ("This username or email address is not available. Choose another one.", 409, no field named) for a taken username or email on every tenant-facing path: administrator's "create user", invitations (email of another tenant; accepting when the email was taken meanwhile) and sign-up. Usernames stay unique platform-wide. Platform staff screens keep their own message.
3. **Lockout (DT-204) — done.** Failures counted per account + IP address (`Application/Identity/SignInThrottle.cs`, in server memory): 5 from one address within 15 minutes block that address for that account for 15 minutes; the per-account backstop (any source) is 20 failures → 15-minute lock on the user row. While blocked or locked every step gives its ordinary failure (`auth.signInFailed`, or `auth.twoFactorInvalid`, `auth.passwordWrong`) — never "locked, N minutes"; the hash cost is paid anyway so timing does not tell either. Every lock is audited (`SignInLocked`, with the address, "blocked for address …" or "account locked for everyone"). An administrator's unlock, an administrator's new password and a password reset clear every address's block. Note: a restart clears address blocks (the backstop stays); a scale-out host would need a shared cache (hosting not chosen, DEF-01).
4. **Forced password change with 2FA (DT-205) — done.** Password → 2FA code → new password → signed in (no second code). `POST /auth/2fa/verify` now answers like sign-in (`SignInResponse`: "done" with the profile, or "changePassword"); the change step accepts the new stage `password-2fa` and completes the sign-in. Users without 2FA unchanged (password → new password → offer/none).
5. **Receipt-number search index (DT-206) — done.** Migration `Rev6_ReceiptNoSearch` (revision 6 §B5, "Extra indexes" below). NFR-01 timing: `/trips?q=499999` on 500,000 trips **999–1,119 ms → 422 ms** (full API run).
- **Tests:** API 197/197 (full run; new: `AccountPageTests` — the three sign-in screens rendered and posted over HTTP; lockout per address and backstop with audit in `IdentityTests`; 2FA-before-change and verification-while-signed-in in `UserNameSignInTests`; generic message assertions updated in `IdentityTests`, `AdministrationTests`, `UserNameSignInTests`); unit 4 + 181, UI 182, infrastructure 49 — all pass. The test host takes the client address from `X-Test-Client-IP` (test project only).
- **Visual check** (`VISUAL_ONLY=account`, 1366/1920, both densities): clean in AR and EN; new states `sign-in#failed` and `verify-email#sign-in` added to the account run; seen by eye.
- OpenAPI re-exported (490 operations; `/auth/2fa/verify` response) and linted; idempotent database script regenerated. No help page describes sign-in or user administration, so none changed.

1. **Two-step verification management — done (DT-210).** `Application/Identity/TwoFactorSettingsService.cs`, `AuthService.VerifyStepUpAsync`, `TotpCode.cs` (RFC 6238 check of the pending key; HMAC-SHA1 analyzer rule suppressed with justification — the algorithm of every authenticator app), endpoints under `/me/2fa`, Profile: box "Two-step verification" (status, since, codes left; Turn on / Change authenticator app / New recovery codes / Turn off — disabled with the reason when required), one dialog for the proof (password + code, or "Use a recovery code instead"), new-app QR + key + confirm, codes shown once. - **Security review (item 1, independent read-only review + fixes):** no step-up bypass found. Fixed from the review: the pending app change is bound to the starting session and dropped after 5 wrong codes (a stolen cookie of another session could otherwise guess for 10 minutes); one answer for a wrong password or code, both values required first (no "password right" signal; a wrong password never uses up a recovery code); email notice failures logged, never undoing the change/session; staff session list uses the staff timeout. Checked OK: antiforgery on all new POSTs, 2FA-off users cannot reach change/codes, staff/required checks before the proof, disabling clears key and codes, constant-time code comparison, audit on every change, HTML-encoded notice, forwarded-header trust, session cache bounded and revocation immediate. **Open (both also applied to the existing sign-in) — fixed on the owner's decision of 19 Oct 2026, DT-212:** (a) an authenticator code can be used again within its 30-s window (Identity does not remember the last used time step) — fix: store the last accepted step per user and refuse older/equal ones (custom token provider, small schema change); (b) one recovery code could be spent twice by two simultaneous requests — fix: conditional update `WHERE UsedAt IS NULL` with a row-count check. The reviewer's "High" item is the owner's local `appsettings.json` (real `UrlTokens:Key`), never committed (already an open issue).
- **Tests:** API 211 (+12: `TwoFactorManagementTests` 8, `SessionLifetimeTests` 4), UI 198 (+8 `ProfilePageTests`; the help-page convention test now also accepts FR-named pages), unit 4 + 181, infrastructure 49 — all pass. - **Two open 2FA points fixed (DT-212, owner 19 Oct 2026):** an authenticator code works once (replay-safe provider, `core.Users.TwoFactorLastStep`, migration `Rev6_TwoFactorLastStep`, revision 6 §B6); a recovery code is spent once even by simultaneous requests. Tests: `CodeReuseTests` (reuse refused at sign-in and in the profile, an older code refused after a newer one, 4 simultaneous requests with one authenticator code / one recovery code → exactly 1 succeeds). API 215, UI 198, unit 4 + 181, infrastructure 49 — all pass (the API run takes ~15 min: the test helper waits for fresh codes like a person); E2E sign-in flows 9.1 and the account visual pages pass with the new E2E helper. 2. **Approve / Refuse failed with "غير موجود" — done; critical isolation bug found and fixed (DT-213).** Cause: the tenant's list query (`SupportAccessService.ListAsync`) used `IgnoreQueryFilters()` in name sub-queries, which in EF Core switches the tenant filter off for the whole query: tenant 3 (owner `aliabdelqader311@gmail.com`, also named "Ali Transportation") was shown tenant 2's request; approve/refuse looked it up with the tenant filter and answered 404. Fix: explicit tenant predicate on the list and on every look-up, names in separate queries; specific translated errors (`support.requestNotFound`, `support.notRequested`, `support.notOpen`); the page reloads the list after such a failure. After a decision the console and the tenant list show the window (from/to, decided by) or "Refused" with its reason (from the decision's audit row, no schema change); the requesting staff member is emailed (DT-215). Security review `docs/reviews/2026-10-19_Support_Access_Security_Review.md` (sweep: one other nested `IgnoreQueryFilters`, `InvoicePrintService`, not a leak — suggestion only).
3. **Console badge "بيئة الإنتاج" — done (DT-216).** Only a wrong label: the badge text was fixed in `TopBar.razor`. The app really runs as Development locally (the only launch profile sets `ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development`; under Production the empty `ConnectionStrings:MasterDb` of `appsettings.json` would stop it). `/me` now returns the host environment to platform staff and the badge shows Development / Staging / Production (other names as they are).

## Tenant isolation hardening after SA-1 (owner request 19 Oct 2026; DT-217…DT-219 accepted 20 Oct)
Testing rule (owner): no full E2E; the new guard and sweep plus all API, unit and infrastructure tests; build with zero warnings. Details and the allow-list: `docs/reviews/2026-10-19_Support_Access_Security_Review.md`, section "Hardening after SA-1".

Fixed to pass it: 17 look-ups now lift only the soft-delete filter, `IssuedNames` lifts nothing, `SessionService` outer lift made explicit (same behaviour). **A new lift in tenant code now needs an allow-list entry with its reason, or the unit tests fail.**
2. **Cross-tenant sweep — done (DT-218).** `tests/DirectService.Transportation.Api.Tests/TenantIsolationSweepTests.cs`: 498 operations from the route table — 146 company reads and 10 tenant/profile reads scanned (534 own reads in both companies, all scanned, none binary-only), 75 record reads and 117 record actions called with tenant B's real token (192 calls, all 404), 866 reads with a query filter naming B's record, 29 provider-console routes (404 to tenants), 105 own writes without a record, 16 anonymous/webhook. No leak of tenant data. Fixed from it: the budget import template accepted another tenant's budget token (no data; now 404). Accepted: idempotent lock release (204) for a lock the caller cannot see, checked to leave the other tenant's lock in place. Checked against SA-1 put back: the sweep fails. **A new endpoint is swept automatically; a new route parameter needs a line in `EntityOf` (or `Values`), otherwise the sweep fails.**

## Owner testing of 20 Oct 2026 — support session ended while open (DT-220 accepted 20 Oct)
Testing rule (owner): no E2E; API tests (requests after an early end and after expiry → 403 with the right reason, never 500), component tests of the staff message and the banner removal, build, visual check of the staff view after the end (EN/AR).
- **Cause of "حدث خطأ في الخادم", reference 0000000D:** not a server error. No exception was logged (Windows event log: no error at 11:10; the tenant's audit log shows the staff member's last request 11:10:18 with 200, the window ended 11:10:26). After the end the dashboard reloaded; the support pipeline answered **404 "not found"** (the company is hidden without a window) and the screen showed every failed load other than 403 as "server error" with the request's reference (`0000000D` = the 13th request on that browser connection).
- **Server:** a staff member with a grant for the subscriber whose window is closed gets **403** with `support.endedBySubscriber` (when + who, from the "End" audit row), `support.windowExpired` (when) or `support.notApproved`; without any grant the company stays 404 (nothing revealed). New job `Workers/SupportWindowWorker.cs` (every 15 s): windows past their end become Expired with an "Expire" audit row. Ending (subscriber) and expiry push `supportEnded` to the staff member's open pages (SignalR, by user) and email the staff member (`EmailTemplates.SupportEnded`). Console/tenant lists: `SupportAccessResponse.EndedBy`; status "Ended by subscriber" with "by X at time", or "Expired".
- **Client:** `ShellState.EndSupport` (from the live message, or any 403 with a support reason, or — on a reload — one request to the company): the page is replaced by `Layout/SupportEndedView.razor` ("The subscriber ended the support session at HH:MM" / "The support window ended at HH:MM", "Back to provider console"), the support banner disappears, the company is left (no company data, requests in flight cancelled).
- **Tests:** API 223 — new in `SupportAccessTests`: early end → 403 `support.endedBySubscriber` (endedAt, endedBy) on **every** company read (no 500), live push, email, "End" audit row, console "ended by"; expiry → 403 `support.windowExpired`, job closes it once ("Expire" audit row, push, email); not approved → 403 `support.notApproved`, no grant → 404; the page address `/c/{company}` is the app (200 HTML). Two unrelated tests failed in the full run under load and passed alone (`CodeReuseTests` simultaneous recovery codes; PDF print engine 503). UI 213 (+5 `SupportEndedTests`: live end, 403 on the next request, reload after the end, another subscriber's end ignored, AR/EN text; banner, menu, company switcher and company figures gone), unit 183 + 4, infrastructure 49 — pass. Build: 0 warnings, 0 errors. ## Owner testing of 20 Oct 2026 — four items + CI (DT-221…DT-225 accepted 21 Oct)
Testing rule (owner): no full E2E; targeted API and component tests; build with zero warnings; visual check only of the sign-in expired message, the e-invoicing settings page and the setup checklist (EN/AR, 1366/1920, with timeout and `--global-timeout=600000`); security review of item 1. Never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`.
1. **Expired account forms and the key ring — done (DT-221).** Cause: an antiforgery token is bound to the signed-in user — a sign-in page opened while anonymous is refused after the same browser signs in (or out) in another tab, or after the browser lost its antiforgery cookie; the Razor endpoint then writes its raw developer text (blank 400 in Production). The key ring **was persisted** (one key since 8 Oct 2026 in `src/…Web/.local/keys`, DPAPI, stable name) but relative to the process working directory — now under the content root (`Web/Security/DataProtectionSetup.cs`), certificate protection at rest when `DataProtection:CertificatePath` is set, Production on Linux refuses unprotected keys (`DataProtection:AllowUnprotectedKeys` to opt out); documented in `docs/step9/Setup_and_Run.md` §4 and the Dockerfile. `Web/Pipeline/ExpiredFormMiddleware.cs`: a refused post to any account page becomes a GET of the page (handler never runs) → fresh token, banner "This page expired. Please try again." (`acct.pageExpired`, `AuthCard`), email/username/names kept for same-site posts (`AccountPage.Kept`), never the password; later sign-in steps send back to sign-in with `expired=1`. In the app: one silent retry as before, a second refusal shows `shell.pageExpired` with "Reload" (`ShellState.PageExpired`, `AppShell`). Security review: `docs/reviews/2026-10-20_Expired_Form_Security_Review.md` (no open findings). Tests: API `ExpiredFormTests` 15 + `DataProtectionSetupTests` 4 (1 runs on Linux only), `AccountPageTests` 3, UI `PageExpiredTests` 3 — pass; build 0 warnings; visual `VISUAL_ONLY=sign-in#expired` (new test "Visual — sign-in page expired") clean AR/EN 1366/1920, seen by eye.
2. **Unreliable tests — done (DT-222).** *Simultaneous recovery codes:* a real bug — the request with the right code failed with 409 when a simultaneous wrong attempt had saved the same user row first (reproduced: 3 of 4 class runs failed, the answers showed 409 `concurrency.conflict` for the winner), and simultaneous wrong attempts were not all counted towards the lockout. Fixed: `Application/Identity/UserRowSave.cs` re-reads the row on a conflict and applies the change again (failure count and lockout, success, the session's last sign-in). New tests `CodeReuseTests`: a right code among six wrong ones signs in (3 rounds), eight simultaneous wrong attempts all counted; class 5 of 5 runs green. *Invoice PDF "engine unavailable":* not reproduced in a loaded run; from the code the 503 comes only from the browser failing — a browser or page dying during a print under memory pressure (reproduced by killing it mid-print: `TargetClosedException`) or a cold start over the library's 30 s launch limit. Fixed in `Infrastructure/Printing/ChromiumPdfRenderer.cs`: one more render on a fresh browser, launch timeout `Printing:LaunchTimeoutSeconds` (120). New `Infrastructure.Tests/PdfRendererTests` (4; the mid-print test fails without the fix). **Full API suite twice in a row:** run 1 — 242 passed, 1 skipped (Linux-only check), 1 failed: the NFR-01 500,000-trip timing test (1,083 ms, limit 1,000 ms; the known load-sensitive one, see "Open issues"); run 2 — 243 passed, 1 skipped, 0 failed. Recovery-code and PDF tests passed in both.
3. **E-invoicing registration type — done (DT-223).** The list offers only "Income tax" and "General sales tax"; saving "Special" is refused (`einvoice.specialNotSupported` on `registrationType`, the connector's refusal kept). The chosen type is described under the list (`eis.regHelp.*`). Checked against the JoFotara builder and corrected: income tax sends no tax and **refuses** an invoice that has tax, so the text tells the company to set its tax rate to 0% in Company settings; general sales tax uses the document's rate (company rate, 16% by default, or a receivable line's rate). Help page "E-invoicing submission" updated (both languages). Tests: API `EInvoiceTests` (+1: Special refused and not stored, Income and Sales saved), UI `EInvoiceSettingsPageTests` 3 (two options, description follows the choice, AR/EN) — pass; visual `settings/e-invoicing` clean AR/EN 1366/1920, seen by eye.
4. **Posting-key codes on screen — done (DT-224).** Setup checklist "Account mapping": "14 posting accounts still need to be linked to your chart of accounts" (Arabic: "14 من حسابات الترحيل لم تُربط بعد بدليل الحسابات") and a closed "Show the list" with the Account mapping page's names (`SetupStep.Items`). Same names (`PostingKeys.Names`) in the posting-disabled refusal (`posting.keysMissing`, also the stored reason of retried pending postings), the AP/AR first-receivable refusal and the shell banner (`AccountingStatusResponse.MissingPostingKeyNames`); wording "posting accounts … linked to the chart of accounts". Searched: audit log, dashboard reminders, notifications, role editor — already translated. Tests: API `OnboardingTests` (+2, EN/AR: count sentence, names, no `TRN.`/`ACC.`, banner names), `InvoiceTests` (refusal names "Transport revenue", no codes), accounting/AP-AR/dashboard classes — 48 pass; UI `SetupChecklistPageTests` 1 — pass; visual `/setup` clean AR/EN 1366/1920.
5. **GitHub CI run #1 — fixed, not pushed (DT-225).** Cause: the UI test project had no `Microsoft.Testing.Extensions.CodeCoverage`; its test host refused the workflow's `--coverage` options → "Zero tests ran", "Handshake failures", exit code 5 (reproduced locally: any option the host does not know gives exactly that). Now `tests/Directory.Build.props` gives every test project the same set-up (xUnit v3 on Microsoft.Testing.Platform, executable, coverage extension, `using Xunit`). - **1. Repayment by the driver / the partner — done (DT-228).** *Settlement screen:* when advances and deductions exceed the selected wages the banner says by how much and "Repayment from the driver" appears: amount (the whole shortfall by default; a part shows "Carried to the next settlement"; above the shortfall or 0 is refused on screen and on the server, `settlement.repaymentRange`), then the usual method (Cash / Bank transfer / CliQ), cash box or bank account and reference (required for bank transfer and CliQ). *Server* (`SettlementRequest.RepaidAmount`): net 0, `RepaidAmount`, `CarriedForward`; the rest becomes a new open advance ADV- with method "Carried" (`CarriedFromSettlementId`; no money moved, no own entry), which the next settlement picks up; it cannot be edited or voided alone (`advance.carried`). One STL entry: Dr payable/expense (wages), **Dr cash box / bank (repaid)**, Cr driver advances (settled − carried; a debit when deductions above the wages are carried), Cr deductions. Void reverses the entry and voids the carried advance (refused while a later settlement settled it: `settlement.carriedSettled`); audited ("CarriedForward", "SettlementVoided", the new columns in the readable history). *Shown:* settlement view ("Repaid by driver: amount (method, reference)", "Carried as open advance ADV-…" with link), settlement print (lines in the totals + a receipt voucher annex "Received from"), carried-advance view/print ("Carried advance — rest of settlement STL-…"), driver statement (line "Repaid by driver: 20.000 (Cash)" increasing the balance; carried advances are not counted twice), accounting dashboard reminder "Driver balances carried to the next settlement after a partial repayment (n)" with one line per carried advance including "Repaid by driver: …" (`AccountingReminder.Items`, readers of driver accounts). *CliQ:* already offered and stored (bank-type, with reference) on receipts, advances, settlements, partner payments and AP/AR payments — nothing to add. *Partners:* a paid trip voided or made cheaper is **no longer refused** (`trip.partnerOverpaid` removed from the trip rules); the partner account then shows "X owes the company Y" with "Record repayment" (`PartnerPaymentRequest.Kind` = Repayment, at most what it owes, `partnerPayment.repaymentExceedsOwed`), Dr cash / bank, Cr partner payable, same PPY- numbering, receipt voucher, statement line "Repaid by the partner"; void reverses. **Database:** migration `Rev6_SettlementRepayment` (revision 6 §B7). Help pages "Driver advance and settlement" and "Partner payment" updated (both languages). Tests: API new `SettlementRepaymentTests` (4: full cash repayment — refusals, entry, account/statement, print, void; partial CliQ — reference required, carried advance, entry, dashboard reminder, next settlement, void order; bank transfer with deductions above the wages; partner repayment cash/bank/CliQ with entries, statement, void) + `CashTests`, `AccountingDashboardTests`, `TripTests` — 25 pass; Application 183, Infrastructure 53 (audit vocabulary coverage) — pass; UI `BillingPageTests` (+2: repayment section AR/EN with default, part, refusal and the request; no section for a positive net) — 12 pass.
- **2. Profile: name, username, email — done (DT-229).** *Name / username:* read-only in the profile ("Changed by a user administrator"); the Owner and Administrators (preset roles in the company, checked on the server — a custom role with users.manage is refused, `user.ownerOrAdministratorOnly`) see "Edit name and username in Users & roles", which opens their own row. Sign-in and password reset keep the old address until the account's user, **signed in**, confirms (`/confirm-email-change` page or `POST /auth/confirm-email-change`, the address shown before the button); then email set and verified, **new security stamp** (earlier links such as a reset to the old address die; this session's cookie re-issued, other sessions end) and a notice to the old address ("Your email was changed… If this wasn't you, contact your administrator", new address masked). An Owner / Administrator starts a change for another user in Users & roles ("Change email", `POST /companies/{c}/users/{u}/email`; their own via the profile, `user.emailOwnInProfile`), shown as "Waiting for the user to confirm: …" with cancel; it takes effect only when that user confirms. ## Owner decisions of 21 Oct 2026 on DT-228 — paid partner trips; email-change limits (DT-230 accepted 21 Oct)
Testing rule (owner): API tests for options a, b, c, permission refused, the daily email limit; component tests for the warning dialog and the three options; build with zero warnings; the CI test commands locally; visual check of the partner trip void dialog and the partner statement (EN/AR); no E2E.
- **Paid partner trip (DT-230).** Voiding a partner trip, moving it to another partner or making it cheaper after the partner was paid (the partner would then be paid more than its trips cost) needs **drv.settle** (the partner-payment permission; otherwise 403 `trip.partnerPaidPermission`), a reason and a choice; without a choice the server answers 409 `trip.partnerPaid` with the amount (`overpaid`), the trip's cost and the partner's last payment (`paymentId`, number, amount), nothing saved. The trip screen then shows the dialog `Pages/Trips/PartnerPaidDialog.razor`: warning "This trip was already paid to the partner; the partner will owe the company X", reason, and (AR/EN, each explained): **(a) The partner will return it** — the partner account shows "owes the company X" until a partner repayment (cash / bank transfer / CliQ); **(b) Deduct it from the next payment** — the balance stays negative and the next partner payment(s) set it off automatically (`TRN_PartnerPayments.DeductedAmount`; no money, no journal line; voucher shows "Due for trips before the deduction" and "Deducted…", the statement line "Payment · deducted X…"; the account shows "To be deducted from the next payment: X"); **(c) The payment was not actually made** — opens that payment voucher with its void dialog (own reason, `CashVoidRequest.ForTrip`), warning that a payment covering several trips is voided for all of them (the user may cancel); the void is recorded with the trip and the balance returns to zero, then the trip voids normally ("Back to the trip"). Every choice is stored in the new table `trn.TRN_PartnerOverpayments` (trip, partner, amount, choice, reason, payment for c), in the trip's readable history (void reason "· paid to the partner X: …", "PartnerPaidChange" for edits, "PartnerPaymentNotMade" for c) and on the partner statement (information line "Voided / cheaper after payment: X paid — …"). **Drivers:** a driver who ends up owing the company already works like (b) — the carried advance of DT-228 is an open advance that the next settlement deducts automatically (DT-228 test "partial repayment… next settlement"); no change.
- **Daily limits of administrator-started email changes (DT-230, review F9):** 5 per administrator and 3 per user in 24 hours (from the audit rows "EmailChangeStarted"); 429 `user.emailChangeLimitAdmin` / `user.emailChangeLimitUser` with the number.
- **Database:** migration `Rev6_PartnerOverpayments` (revision 6 §B9): table `trn.TRN_PartnerOverpayments`, column `TRN_PartnerPayments.DeductedAmount`. Help page "Partner payment" updated (both languages).
- **Tests:** API new `PaidPartnerTripTests` (5: (a) refusal with amount and payment, void with Return, owing balance, overpayment row, history, statement line, repayment entry; (b) deduction set off on the next payment with only the money in the entry, voucher, statement, void gives it back; (c) payment voided for the trip — another company's trip not found, recorded with the trip, then the trip voids with no choice; permission refused for a user with trips.void only, unpaid trips still voidable; email daily limits per user and per administrator) — pass; `SettlementRepaymentTests` partner test now chooses "Return"; cash, trip, account-change, security and isolation classes — 43 pass. UI new `PartnerPaidDialogTests` (5: warning and three options AR/EN, Return/Deduct need a reason and send it, Not paid opens the voucher and warns for several trips) — pass. **CI commands locally (Release + coverage):** build 0 warnings; Domain 4, Application 183, UI 239, Infrastructure 53, API 263 (2 skipped: performance check, Linux-only) — all pass; OpenAPI lint valid. **Visual** (new test "Visual — paid partner trip", `VISUAL_ONLY=partner-paid#`, 1366/1920, timeouts): the void dialog, the "not actually made" choice and the partner statement with the overpaid line and the deducted payment — AR/EN clean, 2 passed, seen by eye.

## Owner testing of 21 Oct 2026 — CI failure and three screen fixes (one commit; DT-231…DT-234 accepted 21 Oct)
Testing rule (owner): no E2E; unit / component / API tests of these issues, a focused visual check with `timeout` and `--global-timeout=600000`; the API project run the CI way; never push, never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`.
1. **GitHub CI: TripListMigrationTests (DT-231).** The failing statement was the **rollback**, not an upgrade: the test migrates the shared API test database **down** to `Rev6_PostJournalEntries` (to re-run `Rev6_ReceiptNoSearch` with trips in place), which also rolls back every later migration — `Rev6_SettlementRepayment.Down` re-creates the old `CK_TRN_Settlements_NetPayable` (`Net = Gross − Advances − Deductions ≥ 0`), and a settlement with a driver repayment left by `SettlementRepaymentTests` / `PaidPartnerTripTests` (net 0, shortfall repaid / carried) cannot satisfy it. **Why local passed:** all API test classes share one database, and whether a repayment settlement existed when the migration test ran depends on the order xUnit runs the classes (it differs between this PC and the GitHub runner); reproduced locally by running `SettlementRepaymentTests` and `TripListMigrationTests` together (same error). **Upgrade is safe** (verified): old rows already satisfy the old constraint and the new columns are added as 0 before the new constraint, so they satisfy it — no data step was needed and the constraint is correct (unchanged). **Changes:** (a) the migration tests run on their own databases (`TripListMigrationFactory` → `DirectServiceMaster_MigrationTests`, `SettlementUpgradeFactory` → `DirectServiceMaster_UpgradeTests`; `ApiFactory.DatabaseName`, CI's server and credentials from `DST_API_TEST_DB` kept); (b) `Rev6_SettlementRepayment.Down` and `Rev6_PartnerOverpayments.Down` refuse first with the reason (`THROW 50231/50232`) while repayments, carried advances, partner repayments, paid-trip choices or payment deductions exist — before, the down path failed half-way after dropping the later migrations' data (pending emails, paid-trip choices) on the shared database; (c) new `SettlementUpgradeTests`: settlements (trips, advance, deduction, net paid), a partner payment made before → rolled back to before `Rev6_SettlementRepayment` (columns gone, net kept) → upgraded: RepaidAmount / CarriedForward 0, net unchanged and = gross − advances − deductions, Kind 'Payment', DeductedAmount 0, no carried advances, same driver and partner balances; then a partial driver repayment → rollback refused with the reason, data intact after re-applying; then a paid partner trip voided with "Deduct" → rollback refused by `Rev6_PartnerOverpayments`, nothing rolled back. Revision 6 note: section "Rollback of B7 and B9".
2. **Profile buttons (DT-232).** Cause: `.formbar` was a non-wrapping flex row aligned to the end; three buttons wider than the 380-px card spilled out on the start side (clipped). Now every form bar wraps (`flex-wrap`, buttons at most the bar's width); the profile has "Save preferences" alone under the preferences and "Change password" / "Change email" in their own start-aligned row; the two-step verification buttons use the same wrapping bar; the profile's two columns stack under 1,600 px (`.cols.lp`).
3. **Ctrl+S (DT-233).** Cause: the shared handler (`wwwroot/js/ui.js`, the only shortcut handler — 26 screens show "Ctrl S", plus Ctrl+P / Ctrl+K / "/") compared `event.key`, which is "س" with the Arabic layout. Now `shortcutKey(e)` uses the physical key (`KeyboardEvent.code` "KeyS", "KeyK", "KeyP", "Slash"); Ctrl+Alt (AltGr) is never a shortcut; Ctrl+S always prevents the browser's "Save page"; the focused field is committed (change event) before the save button — re-read after that change — is pressed; the open dialog's button first. Tests: new `tests/e2e/specs/shortcuts.spec.ts` (5, the handler alone in a bare page — no application: Arabic and English layouts and ⌘S, typed value committed before saving, dialog first, disabled / no button still blocks the browser dialog, AltGr ignored, Ctrl+K and "/" by physical key) — pass.
4. **Tables spilling out of cards (DT-234).** Cause: a table card (`Box NoPad`) had the table directly in the card with no sideways scrolling, so a wide table (Bank accounts with a 30-character IBAN) pushed its last columns over the next panel. Now `Box` wraps a NoPad, non-Fill card's content in `.box-x` (scrolls sideways inside the card; Fill cards keep their own scroller); the IBAN is shown in groups of four (`Fmt.Iban`) and may wrap; list-plus-panel pages (`.cols.lp`: Banks & cash boxes, Cost centres, Maintenance, Profile) stack the panel under the lists below 1,600 px, rows sized to their content (a fixed-height grid squeezed them and the second card covered the first — seen in the first visual run and fixed).
**Tests:** API `TripListMigrationTests` (own database) + new `SettlementUpgradeTests` + `SettlementRepaymentTests` together — pass; UI new `LayoutFixTests` (6: profile rows AR/EN, table card scrolls / Fill and padded cards unchanged, IBAN groups) — UI 245 pass; shortcuts 5 pass. **CI commands locally (Release + coverage, as ci.yml):** build 0 warnings 0 errors; Domain 4, Application 183, UI 245, Infrastructure 53, **API 264 — 262 passed, 2 skipped** (performance check, Linux-only), 0 failed; OpenAPI lint valid. Not checked: Linux (the next GitHub run).
**Visual** (new test "Visual — layout fixes of 21 Oct", `VISUAL_ONLY=fix21#`, widths 1100/1366/1920, compact/comfortable, AR/EN): profile (and scrolled to its buttons), Banks & cash boxes with a long IBAN, Cost centres, Maintenance, and a trip edited with Ctrl+S (the note typed and not left is saved) — 2 passed, no findings; seen by eye (side by side at 1920, stacked at 1366/1100).

Decision (owner): keep the side menu (no top bar). Rules: role permissions exactly as before; the DT-233 shortcut handler and the DT-234 layout untouched; no E2E — component tests plus one focused visual check (AR/EN, 1366/1920, `timeout` and `--global-timeout=600000`); never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`. Design skills used: ui-ux-pro-max (navigation / keyboard guidance; its search returned only general rules), my-frontend-design (kept the existing tokens; one new size step for entry icons), design:design-critique and design:accessibility-review (findings below).
1. **Icons on every entry (DT-235).** `NavItem(Key, Icon, Route, AnyOf…)`: every entry of Operations, Billing & cash, Accounting, Reports, Master data, Settings and Tenant has its own icon; the console's "Platform staff" now has its own (`idcard`, was the same as Tenants). 38 icons added to `wwwroot/img/icons.svg` in the existing style (24-px grid, stroke 1.8, round caps): route, trend-up/down, receipt, file-plus/minus, qr, wallet, swap, gauge, tree, book-open, zap, list, in, out, bank, hub, wrench, pie, target, sliders, bookmark, steering, store, box, download, percent, briefcase, link, hash, layout, form, card, buildings, idcard, panel-in/out. Entry icons are 15 px (sections 17 px), decorative (`aria-hidden`), mirrored where directional; the current entry keeps its background and inline-start bar. No icon repeats inside a section (test-enforced).
2. **Collapsible menu (DT-236).** A button beside the logo ("Collapse menu" / "Expand menu", `aria-expanded`) turns the menu into a 60-px icon rail (`--side-w-mini`, `.shell:has(> .side.mini)`, desktop and tablet only — phones keep the drawer). On the rail every section and single entry shows its name beside the icon on hover or focus; a section shows its entries (with their parts) on hover, or with Enter / click (focus moves to the first entry; Escape closes and returns to the section; focus or a click elsewhere closes it). The flyout is `position: fixed` (never clipped by the scrolling menu), placed by `placeFly` / `navFlyouts` in `ui.js` inside the window (also after a resize). The section holding the current page is marked on the rail.
3. **Remembered state (DT-237).** Collapsed / expanded and the open sections (several may be open now) are stored per user on this device: `localStorage["dst.nav.<user id>"]` = `mini=0|1;open=<section keys>`, through the existing `getPref` / `setPref` (try/catch in `ui.js`); an unreadable or unknown value is ignored. The section holding the current page opens by itself when that page is reached (the user can close it again).
4. **Labelled groups (DT-238).** Sections of more than about 8 entries are split (`NavModel.Part`, keys `nav.part.*`):
   - **Accounting** (19): Accounting dashboard (first, outside the groups) · **Entries** — Journal entries, New journal entry, Import journal entries, Journal templates, Automatic & pending entries · **Balances, banks & cash** — Accounts receivable, Accounts payable, Banks & cash boxes · **Statements & analysis** — Trial balance, Profit & loss, Balance sheet, Account statement, Budget vs Actual · **Setup & control** — Chart of accounts, Cost centres, Budgets, Periods, Maintenance zone.
   - **Billing & cash** (8): **Invoicing** — Invoices, New trip invoice, Manual invoice, Credit notes, E-invoicing · **Collections & accounts** — Receipts, Driver accounts, Partner accounts.
   - **Master data** (9): **Parties** — Drivers & trucks, Customers, Partners, Ownership types · **Materials & sites** — Materials, Loading sites, Unloading sites · **Prices** — Tariffs, Monthly site rates.
   - **Settings** (8): **Company setup** — Company settings, Numbering, Account mapping, E-invoicing, Invoice layout designer, Field configuration · **Users & audit** — Users & roles, Audit log.
   - Operations, Reports, Tenant (4 each) and the console (6) stay ungrouped. Groups with no entry for a role disappear with their label. Order changes: Chart of accounts moved to Setup & control; Receivables before Payables; Numbering before Account mapping.
5. **Ctrl+K page search (DT-239).** Menu pages are matched by their Arabic **and** English name (`CommandResult.Terms`, `Loc.Both`), shown in the interface language, up to 8; the list is built from the user's filtered menu, so a page the role cannot open is never found. Record search unchanged.
**Reviews:** accessibility — menu text 6.3:1 or better on the menu background (part labels `#9FB8BD` on `#0A3540` 6.3:1, entries 10.4:1), focus outline visible on rail and flyout, rail names kept for screen readers (visually hidden label), `aria-current` / `aria-expanded`; fixed: `aria-controls` only while the list exists, the collapse button 44 px on touch screens. Open (minor): Escape does not hide a flyout shown only by mouse hover (moving the pointer away does). Critique: hierarchy reads section → part label → entry; the wrap of "Automatic & pending entries" at the comfortable density is accepted.
**Tests:** new UI `NavMenuTests` (19: icons in the sprite and unique per section, icons and current entry AR/EN, labelled parts, several open sections saved per user, collapse with names / flyouts / Enter / Escape, remembered state restored AR/EN, unreadable preference, console icons, Ctrl+K by Arabic and English name, role filtering expanded and collapsed, Ctrl+K never finds a page the role cannot open) — **UI 264 passed, 0 failed**; `shortcuts.spec.ts` 5 passed (handler unchanged); build 0 warnings 0 errors.
**Visual** (new test "Visual — side menu", `VISUAL_ONLY=nav22#`, widths 1366/1920, compact/comfortable, AR/EN): expanded on the trial balance, collapsed rail, Accounting flyout opened with the keyboard, Ctrl+K finding "Trial balance" by its name in the other language — 2 passed, no findings. First runs found two faults, fixed: the keyboard-opened flyout was placed before its entries showed (ran off a 680-px window) and the focus did not always reach its first entry.

## Owner testing of 22 Oct 2026 — technical field names in messages; Escape on hover flyouts (one commit; DT-240…DT-242 accepted 22 Oct)
Testing rule (owner): no E2E; unit / component / API tests of these issues and one focused visual check of the Maintenance zone (AR/EN, `timeout` and `--global-timeout=600000`); never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`.
1. **Maintenance zone: technical message (DT-240).** Cause: with "Delete selected entries" chosen the page asked the server for a preview at once, with no entries; the server refused (`Fail.Field("entryIds", ValidationRequired, "entryIds")`) and the refusal's text — the field's internal name in «» — was shown as the preview. Now nothing is asked until an entry is ticked: the panel shows a hint (`mnt.pickHint`: "Select the entries you want to delete from the list of manual entries; what will be deleted is shown here." / «حدّد في قائمة القيود اليدوية ما تريد حذفه، فيظهر هنا ما سيُحذف.»; with no manual entries: "No posted manual entries."); ticking shows the preview and the confirmation fields as before; a real refusal is a red banner, not grey text. The other three actions need no choice and show their preview at once (checked); their run-time refusals (reason, confirmation word) now name the fields by label (item 2).
2. **No internal field names in messages (DT-241).** Cause: about 200 field names (`entryIds`, `customerId`, `lines[2].accountId`, `deductions[0].note`…) were passed as the «{0}» of the validation messages and formatted unchanged by `ErrorCatalog.Format`; every screen shows the server's text, so the names reached the user. **One shared place:** `Contracts/Errors/FieldLabels.cs` — Arabic and English label of every request field the services name, list items by number ("Line 3 · Account" / «السطر 3 · الحساب», deductions, sites, allocations, trips, advances, entries, report rows/columns), and the party-type codes ("ApArParty" → "Accounting party"). `ErrorCatalog.Format` applies it to every message argument, so the API's problem details (detail and every field message), the server-rendered account pages and import problems all show labels; ordinary values (names, amounts, numbers) are left as they are. Also: a settlement item that is no longer open is named by its place in the list, not by its URL token. Trip, driver and customer field rules already used the configured labels (unchanged).
   **Screens fixed by the sweep** (screens whose server refusals named a field, found by the scan of the services): Accounting — maintenance zone, journal entry form, drafts, journal templates, journal import, opening balances, chart of accounts, cost centres, periods, banks & cash boxes, AP/AR documents, payments and parties, budgets, statements/report filters; Billing & cash — manual and trip invoices, credit notes, receipts (invoice allocations), advances, driver settlements (deductions, items), partner payments; Operations — trip form, trip import; Master data — drivers, customers, partners, materials, sites, ownership types, tariffs, monthly site rates; Settings — company settings, account mapping, numbering, e-invoicing settings, invoice layout, field configuration, users & roles; Reports — report builder, saved reports, dashboard pins; Tenant — subscription, companies, support approvals; Profile and the sign-in / account pages; provider console — tenants, plans, staff, refunds, announcements.
3. **Escape on a hover flyout (DT-242, open point of DT-235…DT-239).** In the collapsed menu, Escape now also hides a section's flyout or name shown because it is pointed at or focused (`ui.js`: one document listener adds `hush` to that section; `app.css` hides it); it shows again once the pointer or focus leaves the section and comes back. A flyout opened with Enter is still closed by the menu (focus back to its section). The DT-233 shortcut handler is unchanged.
**Tests:** Application new `FieldLabelTests` (scans the Application and Web sources for every field name given to `Fail.Field` / `Fail.RuleOn` / `FieldError` / `Validator` and fails when one has no label in both languages — it found five more, now labelled; formatting AR/EN; no raw name in any field message; values kept) — **Application 407 passed**; UI new `MaintenanceHintTests` (6: hint AR/EN without asking the server, preview after ticking, no manual entries, the other three actions) — **UI 270 passed**; API new `FieldMessageTests` (maintenance preview without entries, journal line without an account: no raw name, label present) with the API classes that check messages (Ledger/maintenance, account pages, administration, expired forms, master data, onboarding, paid partner trips, reports, security, support access, platform, accounting, journal drafts, cash, settlement repayment) — **126 passed, 0 failed** (the full API suite was not run); bare-page `side-menu.spec.ts` (6: Escape on hover and on focus, RTL and LTR, Enter-opened flyout left to the menu) and `shortcuts.spec.ts` 5 — 11 passed; build 0 warnings 0 errors.
**Visual** (new test "Visual — maintenance zone", `VISUAL_ONLY=mnt22#`, widths 1366/1920, compact/comfortable, AR/EN): nothing ticked (hint), one entry ticked (preview, reason, confirmation), delete all — 2 passed, no findings; seen by eye. At 1366 px the preview panel is under the lists (DT-234), so the hint is below the fold there.

## GitHub CI of 22 Oct 2026 — a timing-dependent UI test (one commit; DT-243, for owner review)
Rules (owner): no E2E, no visual check; commit locally, never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`.
**Failure:** `SupportAccessPageTests.Request_dialog_tells_same_named_subscribers_apart_and_sends_only_the_one_shown` (line 63) on the Linux runner — `UnknownEventHandlerIdException: There is no event handler with ID '21' … 'onpointerdown'`; the API step never ran.
**Cause (two parts).** (a) `ComboField.OpenList` called `StateHasChanged()` after its debounced search, and every caller is an event handler (input, click, keys), which renders again when it completes — so the found list was drawn twice, the second time a moment later. (b) In bUnit, a render started by asynchronous work runs on the renderer's dispatcher while the test thread goes on; the test waited until the two options appeared (first render), found the second option and fired `pointerdown` — and on the slower runner the second render came in between, replacing the option and its handler id. Locally the gap was too short to hit. The side menu and dialog code (DT-235…DT-242) does not take part: the page is rendered without the shell, and the menu renders nothing on pointerdown.
**Changes.** (a) `Ui/ComboField.razor`: the extra `StateHasChanged()` removed (one render per search; also in the application). (b) `UiTestBase.Act(() => …)`: finds the element and fires its event in one step on the renderer's dispatcher, so no render can come between; returns after the event's synchronous part, as the plain bUnit calls do. **Every** event fired in the UI tests now goes through it: 244 triggers in 22 test files (238 one-line statements wrapped, 6 by hand — three clicks that were inside `WaitForAssertion` now wait, then act; an element from `WaitForElement`; a checkbox found through its label; an optional tick); 11 elements that were found once and used after later renders (`toggle`, `save`, `create`, `method`, `feb`, `input` ×2, `sw`, `dlg`…) became finder functions (`IElement toggle() => cut.Find(…)`), so each use reads the current render; five helper methods that fire events are no longer static.
**Proof:** UI project 20 times in a row the way ci.yml runs it (Release, `--no-build`, `--coverage`, a foreground loop with a time limit): **20 of 20 runs passed, 270 / 270 tests each** (12–35 s a run). **Full API suite** once (Release, coverage, the CI arguments; in five foreground parts by test class because one run takes about 25 minutes on this PC, all 42 classes): **266 tests — 264 passed, 2 skipped** (Linux-only key-protection check; the explicit 500,000-trip timing check), **0 failed**. Not checked: the next GitHub run (Linux).

## Theme instead of display density; two points of DT-240…DT-242 (owner request 22 Oct 2026; one commit; DT-244…DT-246, for owner review)
Rules (owner): no E2E; component tests and one focused visual check (Light and Dark, AR/EN, 1366 px, `timeout` and `--global-timeout=600000`); API classes affected by the field-label change in foreground parts; never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`. Design skills used: ui-ux-pro-max (dark-mode rules: tonal variants rather than inverted colours, every pair checked; its stored palettes are generic, so the website stays the basis), my-frontend-design (brief fixed by the website: restraint, one accent), design:accessibility-review and design:design-critique (findings below).
**Brand reference — read from https://directservicetech.com/#home (its `:root` CSS variables and computed styles, 22 Oct 2026):** dark theme `--bg #0B1416`, `--paper #101B1E`, `--ink #EEF3F3`, `--muted #A6B4B6`, `--line #223034`, `--petrol #17869B`, `--petrol-deep #0A3540`, `--petrol-tint rgba(23,134,155,.16)`; light theme `--petrol #0F4C5C`, `--petrol-deep #0A3540`, `--petrol-tint #E4EDEE`, `--ink #171A1C`, `--muted #5B6570`, `--line #E3E7E6`, `--bg #F6F8F7`, `--paper #FFFFFF`; buttons: petrol fill with white text, ghost buttons with a `--line` border; focus 2 px petrol outline; radius 8–10 px; no gradients; fonts Inter (body), Space Grotesk (headings), IBM Plex Sans Arabic. The site's light values are already our light tokens (unchanged); our fonts already match (Space Grotesk not added: the app keeps one family per language).
1. **Display density removed (DT-244).** The user menu and the profile page no longer offer it; the former "Comfortable" values are the only ones (`--row 40px`, `--ctl 36px`, `--fs 14.5px`); `data-density`, its texts, `ShellState.Density` and the `density` icon are gone; the stored `dst.density` is removed from the browser and never read.
2. **Theme: Follow device (default) / Light / Dark (DT-244).** In the user menu (`menuitemradio`, icons monitor / sun / moon, "Theme · Saved for you on this device") and on the profile page (a select, applied at once, nothing sent to the server). Stored per user on this device (`dst.theme.<user id>` via `getPref`/`setPref`, try/catch); the last choice of the device (`dst.theme`) is applied before the first paint by `wwwroot/js/theme.js`, a blocking script in `<head>` (the CSP allows no inline script) that resolves "system" from `prefers-color-scheme`, follows changes of the device setting, and re-applies itself when enhanced navigation replaces the `<html>` attributes. Sign-in, sign-up and the other account pages follow it. **Tokens in one place:** `tokens.css` holds every colour; the dark block is `:root[data-theme="dark"]` inside `@media screen`, so printing and PDF stay light; print previews and the invoice-layout preview are white documents (`--doc-bg`), as are QR codes. New tokens: `--primary-fill`, `--on-primary`, `--on-tint`, `--err-fill`, `--chart-amber`, `--stamp`, `--doc-bg`, `--side-strong`, `--side-mark`, `--side-hover`, `--side-press`, `--side-scroll`, `--toast-bg/-text/-link`, `--brand-panel(-text)`, `--shadow-panel-ltr`, `--shadow-pop`. Hard-coded colours replaced: 31 in `app.css` (white text on brand fills, side-menu overlays, toast, toggle knob, chart orange, stamp, QR, shadows), the account pages' brand line, both print-preview frames. **Dark palette** (site value → token): `#0B1416` → `--page`; `#101B1E` → `--surface`, `--side-bg`; `#EEF3F3` → `--text`; `#A6B4B6` → `--muted`; `#223034` → `--border`, `--side-line`; `#17869B` → lightened to `#4FB8CC` for `--primary` (links, active tabs, focus, accents) and darkened to `#147A8C` for `--primary-fill` (buttons, switches), because the site's petrol is only 4.1–4.4:1 on its dark backgrounds and 4.3:1 under white text; `#0A3540` → `--brand-panel` (sign-in brand side); petrol tint → `--tint #123940` (selected rows, chips); derived surfaces `--surface-head #142225`, `--surface-filter #17282C` (hover), `--surface-mute #1C2E32`; semantic ok `#5DCB91`/`#0F2A1E`, warn `#E9B455`/`#2B2110`, err `#F4877D`/`#2E1614`, info `#86BCF0`/`#0F2238`; filled danger stays `#B42318`. **Contrast (dark):** text 16.7 (page) / 15.7 (surface) / 13.6 (row hover); muted 8.2 / 7.6 (table head); placeholder 5.6; accent 7.6 (surface) / 5.4 (selected row); white on button 5.0, on danger 6.6; badges ok 7.6, warn 8.4, err 6.9, info 8.0; menu text 13.9, part labels 8.4; toast 12.1; non-text: input border 4.2, focus ring 7.6, chart bars 6.0–6.1 on their track — all ≥ 4.5:1 (text) / 3:1 (non-text).
3. **Field labels only for the field reference (DT-245, open point of DT-241).** `ErrorCatalog.Format` replaced every string argument that matched a field name, so a value equal to a field name (a template called "name", a cash box called "amount") was shown as the label. New `Contracts/Errors/FieldRef`: only a `FieldRef` argument is labelled; plain strings are always shown as they are. 209 calls that passed their own field as an argument now pass `new FieldRef(…)` (found by parsing every `Fail.Field` / `Fail.RuleOn` / `new FieldError`), plus `Validator`, the deduction / quantity / row-version items, party types and party roles (`Supplier` label added), the logo upload; `TripImportService` puts the trip form's label in for a `FieldRef` only.
4. **Maintenance zone at 1366 px (DT-246, open point of DT-240).** The "Choose the manual entries" box shows "No entries ticked" / "Ticked: N — preview updated" (`role="status"`); the first tick (0 → 1) brings the preview into view once it is drawn — `revealStacked` scrolls only when the panel is stacked under the lists (one grid column), smoothly, at once with reduced motion; later ticks do not move the page.
**Tests:** UI new `ThemeTests` (themes in the menu AR/EN with Follow device default and no density; choosing dark saves `theme.u1` and applies it; saved / missing / unreadable choice restored; profile select AR/EN applies without a request; no colour literal in any `.razor` or `app.css`; every token used is defined and the dark rule is screen-only) and 2 more `MaintenanceHintTests` (count AR/EN, reveal on the first tick only) — **UI 283 passed**; Application `FieldLabelTests` (values equal to field names kept AR/EN, `FieldRef` vs value with the same text, a source guard that fails when a call passes its field name as a plain string) — **Application 411 passed**; API new `FieldMessageTests.A_record_named_like_a_field_keeps_its_name_in_the_message` (journal template named "name") with the 30 classes that read message text (FieldMessage, JournalTemplate, AccountPage, ExpiredForm, JournalDraft, LedgerReport, Accounting, SettlementUpgrade, ApAr, Cash, SettlementRepayment, PaidPartnerTrip, Invoice, Trip, Budget, MasterData, Pricing, Report, AccountingDashboard, Collaboration, EInvoice, Administration, Onboarding, Security, SupportAccess, Platform, Staff, DataProtectionSetup, TenantIsolationSweep) in five foreground parts — **199 tests: 198 passed, 1 skipped (Linux-only key protection), 0 failed** (the full API suite was not run); build 0 warnings 0 errors. The laptop restarted during the first API run; the restart left zero-filled DLLs in the test output (PDF tests failed with `print.unavailable`: `Microsoft.IO.RecyclableMemoryStream` could not load), so every `bin`/`obj` was deleted, the solution rebuilt and all parts run again.
**Visual** (the harness's density loop is now Light / Dark, `VISUAL_THEMES`; screenshots in `TestResults/visual/<lang>/<width>-<theme>/`; `print/invoice/<id>` added to the screens): dashboard, accounting dashboard (charts), trips list, new trip, trial balance, banks & cash boxes, maintenance zone (+ `mnt22#` states), profile, sign-in / sign-up / two-step / account pages, invoice print preview — AR and EN at 1366 px, Light and Dark: 4 + 1 + 1 passed, no layout findings; dark screens seen by eye. **Reviews:** accessibility — contrast above, focus ring visible on every surface, badges keep icon + text, theme items are `menuitemradio` with `aria-checked`, the count is a live status, reduced motion respected; critique — layering page < surface < header reads like the website, one accent, print previews stay white inside the dark shell. **Still to note (not changed):** the Ctrl+S / Ctrl+P key chips inside filled buttons keep their existing outlined look (transparent, white text, white border — same in light, 5.0:1 on the button), so they read as a small dark box on the teal; the info banner (trial) stays blue, not petrol, as a status colour; the white print preview is bright beside the dark shell (required: documents stay light); a different user signing in on the same device sees the previous user's theme until the shell loads their own choice (one frame).
**Rules for future screens:** (1) use the colour tokens of `tokens.css` only — no hex, rgb or named colour in a page, component or `app.css` (`ThemeTests` fails otherwise); add a token, with its dark value, when a new colour is needed; (2) the dark theme follows the company website's palette (directservicetech.com) — adjust only where WCAG AA needs it, and check new pairs (4.5:1 text, 3:1 controls).

## Owner testing of 22 Oct 2026, part 1 — screen fixes (one commit; DT-247…DT-249, for owner review)
Rules (owner): no E2E; component tests (events only through `UiTestBase.Act`), one focused visual check (dashboards and two reports, AR/EN, light/dark, 1366 px, `timeout` and `--global-timeout`); never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`.
1. **Accounting dashboard squeezed with its own scrollbar (DT-247).** Cause: the cards' grid had the `fill` class (`flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto`) — made for list screens whose table scrolls inside the page — so it got only the height left under the figures and the month / year boxes and scrolled inside that strip, cutting the cards off. New `.flow` class (`flex: none; min-height: auto; overflow: visible`): the cards take their own height and the page (`.content`) is the only scroller. Applied to every dashboard-like and list-plus-panel page: main dashboard, accounting dashboard, setup checklist, balance sheet (it also had an inline `overflow:auto`), and the `.cols.lp` pages (Banks & cash boxes, Cost centres, Maintenance zone, Profile — DT-234 had stacked them, but they still scrolled inside the page under 1,600 px). The provider console has no dashboard of this kind (Health is a plain page). **Dark colours:** no colour literal on any dashboard (tokens only, `ThemeTests`); pairs computed: text 15.7, muted 8.2, links 7.6 (on surface) / 8.1 (page), revenue / expense bars 7.6 / 7.4, ageing bars on their track 5.8–7.0, reminder badges 6.9–8.4; light: expense bar 4.2, ageing bars 3.7–5.8 (non-text ≥ 3); scrollbars follow `color-scheme: dark`.
2. **Reports: which report is open (DT-248).** The results column starts with the report's code and name (`RPT-04 · Tariff breach report`) and a one-line description of what it shows (new `Description` in the catalogue, Arabic and English, all 24 reports); the open report is marked in the list (tint, a bar on the start side, bold; `aria-current="page"`; an outline in forced-colours mode); the report and its filters are kept in the address (`?report=tariff-breach&from=…&to=…&side=customer`): choosing a report adds a history entry (Back returns to the one before), running with other filters replaces it, and opening an address (refresh, bookmark, Back / Forward) shows that report with those filters; the browser tab is named "RPT-04 · Tariff breach report · Direct Service Transportation" (`PageTitle`). Under 1,100 px the list stacks above the report and both flow with the page; choosing a report then scrolls its title into view (`revealStacked`).
3. **"Side" shown empty (DT-249).** Cause: the screen left every filter it did not know empty, and the server applied its defaults (side = customers, period = this month up to today, as of = today, margin grouped by customer) — so the boxes were empty while the result line named the value. The catalogue now carries each report's defaults (`Defaults`, from the same `PeriodAsync` / today the reports use); the screen fills the boxes with them when a report opens, and again for any box the user empties before running. Other report filters checked: the same fault was in the period and as-of boxes of every standard report (fixed by the same change); trial balance, profit and loss, balance sheet, account statement, ageing and budget vs actual already show the period the server used; list date filters are empty on purpose ("all dates").
**Tests:** UI new `ReportSelectionTests` (6: title and description above the filters, active item with `aria-current`, tab title through `HeadOutlet`, address with filters and a history entry per report, Back reopens the report before and runs it, a bookmarked address with `side=partner`, default values in Side / From / To / Group by / As of, an emptied box refilled on run) and 2 in `LayoutFixTests` (accounting dashboard AR/EN flows with no scroll area; source check of every dashboard and list-plus-panel page and the `.flow` rule) — **UI project 292 / 292**; API `ReportTests` (descriptions and defaults of the catalogue) — 5 / 5.
**Visual** (new test "Visual — dashboards and reports of 22 Oct", `VISUAL_ONLY=rep22#`, 1366 px light/dark, AR/EN): accounting dashboard and main dashboard (whole height too), with an assertion that nothing inside the page scrolls vertically by itself at 1366 and 1920 px; Tariff breach report (title, marked item, address, tab title, Side = Customers, still open after a reload); Driver balances (as of shown) — 2 passed, no findings; seen by eye.

## Owner request 22 Oct 2026, part 2 — system email via Microsoft 365 (one commit; DT-250…DT-253, for owner review; closes DEF-02 for email)
Owner decision: option A (Microsoft 365 through Microsoft Graph, an Entra app with a client secret; no SMTP, no mailbox password). Rules: never real credentials or real emails (a fake Microsoft sign-in / Graph handler in every test), no E2E, focused visual check, never push. Skills used: dotnet-core-expert / secure-code-guardian / test-master (structure, review checklist, tests), ui-ux-pro-max (its search tool needs Python, not installed — its built-in priority rules applied: visible labels, errors at the field, status as icon + text, live result region) and my-frontend-design (the console's existing restraint).
1. **Sender behind an interface (DT-250).** `IEmailSender` (every flow) now writes to the outbox; each mode is an `IEmailTransport`: `FileEmailTransport` (Development — the `.eml` files as before, 8-bit readable MIME) and `GraphEmailTransport` (Microsoft 365: Azure.Identity `ClientSecretCredential` — already in the dependency graph through Microsoft.Data.SqlClient, 1.17.1 — and an HttpClient POST of the base64 MIME message to `/users/{mailbox}/sendMail`). Another provider = a new mode + transport. One MIME builder for all modes (plain text + HTML + inline logo `cid:logo`, `Auto-Submitted`, encoded-word subject and sender name with control characters removed, addresses re-validated). Errors become kinds the console words in plain language (wrong tenant / client / secret, expired secret, missing permission, mailbox not allowed, mailbox or domain not in this Microsoft 365, recipient refused, throttled, unavailable, network …); a token with the tenant-wide `Mail.Send` role gives a warning.
2. **Outbox and worker (DT-251).** `core.EmailOutbox` (revision 6 §B10): recipient, type, language, times, status, attempts, error kind and reason; the message only encrypted (Data Protection) and only while it may still be sent — wiped when sent, and on failure when it holds a single-use link (DB check). `EmailOutboxWorker` → `EmailDispatcher`: claims due rows with UPDLOCK/READPAST, at most 30 a minute (database-counted, `Email:PerMinute`), Retry-After of 429/503 pauses the queue, back-off 1 min / 5 min / 15 min / 1 h / 4 h / 12 h, Failed after 6 attempts; a send interrupted mid-way is Failed "may have been delivered", never repeated on its own (no duplicates); platform staff are emailed about failures (at most once in 6 h) and 30 / 7 days before the secret expires; addresses removed after 180 days and with a deleted tenant's data; 5 emails of a kind per address an hour (more are Suppressed). Resend: the same message (notices), or a new link from the flow (password reset, verification, staff invitation); company invitations and email-change confirmations say who must ask again. `create-staff` sends its email before exiting.
3. **Console screen "System email" (DT-252).** `/console/system-email` (menu after Platform staff): mode, Microsoft 365 fields with a write-only secret ("saved on / expires on"), sender name, reply-to; changes of mode / mailbox / tenant / client / secret are **Pending** until a test email with them succeeds (active and pending shown); every save asks for a fresh authenticator code (`AuthService.VerifyFreshCodeAsync`, used once, counts towards lockout); test email with the reason in plain words and technical details; status (working / failing, last success, waiting, failed, limit); delivery log with status filter, recipient search, paging and Resend; a banner 30 days before the secret expires, and a banner on every console page (`Layout/SystemEmailAlert`) when emails failed or the secret expires. Audit: `CORE.SystemEmailSetting` Create / Update / Cancel / Activate / Deactivate / EmailTest (secret `***`), `CORE.EmailOutbox` Resend; words in `AuditVocabulary`, field labels in `FieldLabels`. Arabic / English, RTL / LTR, light / dark, tokens only.
4. **Email templates (DT-253).** One layout (`Notifications/EmailLayout`): logo, the recipient's language first (subject too) and the other language below, a button plus the same link to copy, plain-text alternative, footer with the company name and the reply-to address (or "automatic email"), always light (`color-scheme: light only`, fixed white card). All 15 emails use it (the tenant-deletion notice was a hand-made message before); call sites pass the recipient's language.
5. **Owner guide** `docs/step9/Email_Microsoft365_Setup.md` (linked from Setup_and_Run §5): shared mailbox + blocked sign-in, Entra app, Exchange RBAC for Applications scoped to `no-reply@` with removal of any tenant-wide grant and `Test-ServicePrincipalAuthorization`, client secret, SPF / DKIM / DMARC, limits, rotation, changing mailbox or domain. **Security review** `docs/reviews/2026-10-22_System_Email_Security_Review.md`: no open finding; fixed during the review — F1 test emails capped at 10 per 10 minutes (they bypass the queue's per-address limit), F2 the audit interceptor assumed every modified row is soft-deletable.
**Database:** migration `Rev6_SystemEmail` — two new tables only (`core.SystemEmailSettings`, `core.EmailOutbox`), nothing existing changes; its Down refuses (THROW 50233) while settings are saved or emails wait. `SettlementUpgradeTests` re-applies it after the refused rollback of `Rev6_PartnerOverpayments` (it has no data there).
**Tests:** Infrastructure `GraphEmailTransportTests` (15: success with MIME parts and token reuse, tenant-wide warning, AADSTS wrong secret / expired / client / tenant, 403 / 404 / 401 / 400 kinds, 429 and 503 Retry-After, header injection, folding) — project 68 / 68; Application `EmailTemplateTests` (every template AR / EN layout, single-use marks, reply-to footer, encoding) — 436 / 436; API `SystemEmailTests` (6, own database `DirectServiceMaster_EmailTests`: staff-only 404, fresh code, secret never returned / audited, header injection refused, pending → active only after a successful test, retry with Retry-After, never twice, interrupted, failed after 6 attempts with link wiped and staff told, resend new link / same, expiry warning, flood suppressed, development files) plus the affected classes in three parts — **117 tests: 116 passed, 1 skipped** (Linux-only key check); UI `SystemEmailPageTests` (10) — **UI 302 / 302**; Domain 4 / 4. OpenAPI summaries added for the six endpoints (`SecurityTests`); the exported `docs/api/openapi-v1.json` was **not** re-exported (needs the running host).
**Visual** ("Visual — system email", `VISUAL_ONLY=mail22#`, 1366 px light/dark, AR/EN; the E2E host in Development mode — nothing leaves the machine): opened, Microsoft 365 chosen, the fresh-code dialog, a test email with the log — 2 passed, no findings; seen by eye.

## Owner request 25 Oct 2026 — journal line reference and tax (one commit; DT-254…DT-260, for owner review)
Rules (owner): no E2E; tests for tax calculation and rounding, tax lines on both sides, balance with tax, 0 % code, required tax account, tax-code rules, line reference default and search, reversal / void / copy / draft / template / import with tax, migration on existing entries, the default-tax-accounts button; one focused visual check (journal form with taxed lines and the lists open, Settings → Tax codes; AR/EN, light/dark, 1366 px, `timeout` and `--global-timeout=600000`); never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`.
1. **Reference on every line (DT-254).** New column Reference on the journal form (after the account); a new line starts with the entry's Reference, and lines that still show the previous entry Reference follow a change of it (a line given its own keeps it); a line sent without one takes the entry's when posted. Search (journal list, Ctrl+K) finds header and line references; Ctrl+K shows the matching line reference. Account statement (one account and several) shows the line reference and has a new **Reference** filter (line or entry reference, applied like the cost-centre filter). Entry view, export (new columns), drafts, templates, import (`LineReference`), copy, edit, void and reversal carry it.
2. **Tax code on every line (DT-255).** Columns Tax code (list, "No tax" first), Rate, Tax, Gross (read-only) and Tax account (list of **tax accounts only**, never defaulted; shown only for a rate above 0 %). Debit / credit = amount before tax; tax = amount × rate, half-up to the currency's decimals (3 for JOD); gross = amount + tax. Footer: "Net (before tax)" with the tax total, "Total with tax" with the balance badge ("Balanced including tax" / "Difference including tax"). The automatic tax lines posting will add are previewed (muted rows, "Automatic", "Tax of line n"). Posting (`PostingEngine`): for each taxed line with tax > 0 an automatic line on the tax account, **same side**, same cost centre and reference, note "ضريبة المبيعات 16% على السطر 2 / VAT 16% on line 2", numbered after the entered lines, linked (`TaxForLineId`), never editable on its own (edit = reverse + re-post from the entered lines); 0 % code: no tax account, no tax line; the entry must balance **with** the tax lines — new message `journal.unbalancedWithTax` names the tax on each side and says it is added on the same side. Refusals: `journal.taxAccountRequired` (field `lines[i].taxAccountId`), `journal.taxAccountInvalid`, `journal.taxCodeInactive`. Drafts (totals with tax), templates (template screen has the same columns), import (`LineReference`, `TaxCode`, `TaxAccount`; row errors name the columns through `FieldLabels`: "Unknown Tax code: «…»"), edit, void and reversal (mirrored lines keep the link) carry tax. Invoices, credit notes, supplier bills and JoFotara unchanged.
3. **Tax accounts (DT-256).** Account flag "Tax account" in the chart form (never a control account, DB check), a Tax column in the chart; template (new companies): 1105 (input VAT) and 2104 (output VAT) marked, **5207 "VAT expenses (non-recoverable)"** added under 52 and marked (49 template accounts). Existing companies: nothing automatic; a banner on the chart (and a button in the line's tax-account cell when the chart has none) — **"Add the default tax accounts"**: per kind (liability / asset / expense) without an active tax account, the template's own 1105 / 2104 / 5207 is marked when it is there (same code, type and one of its names), otherwise a new account is added with the next free code under the group (11, 21, 52; else the first top-level account of the type that can take sub-accounts).
4. **Settings → Tax codes (DT-257).** `/settings/tax-codes` (menu after Numbering, `core.settings.manage` like the other settings): code, rate %, Arabic and English description, order, status, "In use"; add, edit, deactivate / activate, delete with a reason. Seeded per company (editable): VAT_JO_0, 1, 2, 4, 5, 7, 8, 10, 16 %. A code on posted entries cannot be deleted nor change its code or rate (fields read-only with the reason; API `taxCode.inUse`). Active codes for the journal: `GET accounting/tax-codes/options` (acct.post / postentry / view, je.view, settings.manage). Audit `ACC.TaxCode`; words in `AuditVocabulary` (TaxCodeId, TaxAccountId, TaxAmount, Rate, IsTaxAccount; TaxForLineId hidden).
5. **Copy (DT-258).** There was no copy of a journal entry; added: "Copy" on a manual entry's view → new entry dated today with the description, Reference, lines, line references and tax codes (not the automatic tax lines, which are made again).
6. **Database (DT-259).** Migration `Rev6_JournalLineTax` (revision 6 §B11): table `acc.TaxCodes`; `acc.EntryLines` Reference, TaxCodeId, TaxRate, TaxAmount, TaxAccountId, TaxForLineId (+ checks, FKs, filtered indexes); `acc.Accounts.IsTaxAccount`. Existing entries: line Reference copied from the entry's Reference (chosen so old and new entries read alike), no tax code; tax codes seeded for every company; no account added or marked. Down refuses (THROW 50259) while any line carries a tax code. `database/MasterDatabase_Idempotent.sql` regenerated.
7. **Combo lists in Arabic (DT-260, found by the visual check).** `floatList` (ui.js) kept an open list inside the window only in LTR; in RTL a long list opened near the left edge (the tax account of a line, after the grid scrolled) ran off the window.
