## Owner request 25 Oct 2026, part 2 — CI failure of SystemEmailTests and RPT-23 (one commit; DT-261, DT-262, for owner review)
Rules (owner): no E2E; never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`; don't wait for GitHub runs.
1. **GitHub CI: `SystemEmailTests.New_settings_become_active_only_after_a_successful_test_email` (DT-261).** Not another class: `SystemEmailTests` already runs alone on its own database (`DirectServiceMaster_EmailTests`, collection `SystemEmail`, outbox and settings deleted before each test, background workers off). **Cause:** `GraphEmailTransport` is a singleton that keeps one `ClientSecretCredential` (and its access token) per tenant / client / secret. Every test uses the same fake app, so when a test that activated Microsoft 365 ran first, the "wrong secret" test email reused the kept token: the fake sign-in's refusal was never asked, the email was sent → `Assert.False(failed.Sent)` failed. **Why only CI:** xUnit v3 orders the tests of a class by IDs that include the assembly path — `bin/Release` on Linux gives another order than `bin/Debug` here. **Reproduced** locally with a Release build (same assertion). **Product fix:** a test email now always asks Microsoft sign-in for a **new token** (`EmailTransportSettings.FreshToken`), so "pending becomes active only after a successful test" really proves the app and secret work now — before, a secret revoked in Entra (or pending settings with a kept app / secret) could "pass" on a token kept for up to an hour; the new credential then replaces the kept one, and a refused sign-in drops the kept one (queued emails meet the problem too). **Concurrency (checked, correct, now tested):** activation happens in one transaction on the tracked pending row, whose row version EF checks — settings changed while a test email is on its way are never activated by it (409, nothing activated, the new pending stays untested), and two test emails at the same moment activate once (the other answers 409). **Tests:** `ResetAsync` also forgets kept tokens (`GraphEmailTransport.ClearTokens`); new: a refused secret is found despite a kept token; a change during a test email (fake Graph `Hold` gate) → 409, nothing activated; two tests at once → one activation, one Active row. **Repeated runs:** SystemEmailTests + Staff, Platform, Identity, AccountChange, SupportAccess (43 tests) and `GraphEmailTransportTests` (15), **10 times in a row in Release: 10/10 green** (0 failures). **Full API suite** (Debug, four foreground parts with time limits): **314 tests, 312 passed, 0 failed, 2 skipped** (explicit tests, incl. the 500k performance test).
2. **RPT-23 sub-ledger reconciliation (DT-262).** **Banner levels:** red ("there are differences between control accounts and sub-ledgers") only when a control account differs from its party balances; **amber** "Control accounts match the party balances. Some amounts come from manual journal entries (or the opening entry), not from documents." when only documents differences exist; green otherwise; a second amber line names accounts whose documents difference is not explained by manual / opening entries (check pending postings). API: `ReconciliationReport.Level` (Ok / Documents / Differences); `Reconciled` = every control account equals its parties. **Help:** a "How to read this report" box explains ledger balance, party balances, difference, balance from documents and documents difference. **Drill-down:** "Documents difference" is a button → the manual journal entries and the opening entry behind it (date, entry link, kind, description, amount on the account) and the split "manual entries · opening entry · unexplained"; voided / reversed entries and their reversals cancel out and are not listed. **Consistency:** every control account has documents figures — rebuilt from the documents where they can rebuild it (as before), otherwise **what documents posted** (automatic entries), marked "as posted by documents" — so 110302 shows 0 / −200 like 110301 shows 0 / 200 (before "—"). **Opening balances:** the product has no per-party opening; the account-level opening entry (no party, DT-24) is **not** a document — listed as "Opening entry" in the drill-down (and, having no party, it is also a real control-vs-parties difference: red); per-party openings entered as AP/AR documents count as documents, entered as manual entries with a party count as manual entries. **Export (Excel / CSV / PDF):** the same figures for every account plus the split columns, the entries behind each documents difference as rows under their account, and the verdict (PDF band; last row in Excel / CSV). **Tests:** API `ReconciliationTests` (2), UI `ReconciliationReportTests` (5: amber not red, red only for a real difference, every figure explained, documents figures on every account, the drill-down with links via `Act`); Application 463, UI 314, Infrastructure 68 passed. **Visual** (`VISUAL_ONLY="rpt23#"`, 1366, AR/EN, light/dark): 12 screenshots, no problems.

## Owner request 26 Oct 2026 — budget screen, profit and loss, Arabic tax names (one commit; DT-263…DT-267, accepted 26 Oct 2026)
1. **Arabic tax names (DT-263, owner decision).** Default tax codes "ضريبة المبيعات العامة n%" (was "ضريبة المبيعات n%"); the VAT expenses account (template 5207 and the "Add the default tax accounts" button) "ضريبة مبيعات غير قابلة للخصم". New companies get them from the template / seed; existing companies through migration `Rev6_BudgetProfitLoss`, **only where the old default text is still there** (a name the user changed is never touched; the button still recognises the old name as the template's own account). Automatic tax lines now read "ضريبة المبيعات العامة 16% على السطر n"; lines posted before keep their stored note.
2. **Profit and loss sections on the chart (DT-264).** The chart had no way to mark cost of sales: new account setting **"Profit and loss section"** (`acc.Accounts.ProfitLossGroup`, revision 6 §B12) — revenue: sales / other income; expenses: cost of sales / operating / other expenses; empty = as the parent account (shown, e.g. "As the parent account (Cost of sales)"), else sales / operating. Template: **51 Direct transport costs = cost of sales** (5101…5105 follow). Existing companies: 51 marked only while it is still the template's own (code, type, name) and has no section. Validation: a section of the account's own type only (`validation.format` on `profitLossGroup`); audited (`AuditVocabulary`), label in `FieldLabels`.
3. **Budget screen: every account at once (DT-265).** Every posting account of the chart is a row (group accounts are headings with subtotals, never editable), the 12 months are columns, plus the row total; "Add line" is gone. Filters (they only choose the rows shown): account type (income and expense — default —, income, expense, balance sheet, existing amounts, all), cost centre (accounts posted with it in the budget year or the year before — new `GET accounting/budgets/cost-centre-accounts`; the budget itself stays company-wide), search by code or name. Tick rows ("Select all", "Clear selection") and **Distribute** a yearly amount (÷ 12 rounded to 3 decimals, December takes the difference), **Fill** the following months from a chosen month (January by default), **Clear**; the helpers act on the ticked rows that are shown. Totals at the bottom: the rows shown, and the whole budget's revenue and expenses per month (figures on top: revenue, expenses, net). Only rows with amounts are sent and stored (server: all-zero lines are dropped on save and import); amounts in rows hidden by the filters are kept and saved. Grid: plain inputs in a row component that renders again only when its row changes (fast with several hundred accounts), sticky header, first column and totals, Enter / Shift+Enter down / up, Tab across, the text selected on focus (`ui.js amountGrid`), Ctrl+S (DT-233), a cell that is not an amount is marked and blocks saving. Balance-sheet accounts may now be budgeted (natural direction: assets debit, liabilities / equity credit); Budget vs Actual unchanged (revenue and expenses). Lines made per cost centre or on a group account earlier (copy by cost centre, import) are **kept as they are** and counted: an info banner with their number and total and "Remove these lines"; sub-accounts of a group with its own company-wide line are read-only (no double counting). New budget: the "one line per cost centre" choice is removed (owner decision: company-wide). New `GET accounting/budgets/grid-accounts`. Help page updated.
4. **Profit and loss, new layout (DT-266).** Sales and revenue → cost of sales → **gross profit** → operating expenses → other income → other expenses → **net profit**, by the account tree: every group expands / collapses (expand all / collapse all); an open group has a total row, a closed one shows its totals. **Columns by** none (one amount; the comparison period stays here) / cost centre (the cost centres with amounts and **"No cost centre"**) / month / quarter / year (first and last column cut to the period), always with a **Total** (= the sum of the columns; the API test checks every row). Period From / To with quick picks this month / this quarter / this year / last year; cost-centre filter with "No cost centre"; **Hide zero rows** (default on). Header and first column sticky, many columns scroll inside the card; tokens only (light / dark), RTL / LTR, print without the inner scroll. API: `ProfitLossQuery.ColumnsBy`, `HideZero`; `ProfitLossReport.Columns`, `Sections`, `GrossProfit`, `Net` (the earlier `Revenue` / `Expenses` lists and totals are still there). Tax lines are ordinary postings on their tax accounts (e.g. 5207 under operating expenses). **Exports** Excel / CSV / PDF with the same columns and grouping (section bands, rows indented, section totals, gross and net profit); PDF **landscape** above six amount columns (`ReportTable.Landscape`).
5. **Drill-down (DT-267).** An amount of an account opens its account statement for that column: the column's dates (month / quarter / year) or the period, and the column's cost centre (or the report's). The statements accept `costCentre=none` (lines without a cost centre) — account statement (several accounts), general ledger, P&L; the account statement takes `?costCentre=` in its address and offers "No cost centre" in its filter.
6. **Tests.** Application: `BudgetProfitLossUnitTests` (distribution and rounding, directions, sections, template, tax names) — **472 passed**. UI: `BudgetGridProfitLossPageTests` (11: all accounts and headings, only non-empty rows saved, filters type / cost centre / search keep hidden amounts, Distribute / Fill / Clear, invalid cell, kept lines and locked rows, P&L sections / gross / net / expand-collapse, columns by cost centre with "No cost centre" and the drill-down address, month columns with quick period, hide zero, export choice, statement for "none", the chart's section field), `BudgetPageTests` updated — **324 passed**, all with `Act`. API: `BudgetProfitLossTests` (9: new names and 51 for new companies, migration data steps change only default texts, section validation, grid accounts / balance-sheet line / all-zero line dropped / Budget vs Actual, cost-centre filter years, sections with group totals, gross and net, hide zero, comparison, every "Columns by" incl. "No cost centre" with totals = sum of columns, statement filters incl. none, exports and landscape); updated `BudgetTests` (asset accounts accepted), `AccountingDashboardTests` (an all-zero line is not kept), `JournalTaxUpgradeTests` (a refused rollback past Rev6_JournalLineTax first rolls back the later migration — re-applied). Affected API classes (16, Debug, foreground parts with time limits): Budget, BudgetProfitLoss, LedgerReport, Accounting, JournalTax, JournalTaxUpgrade, AccountingDashboard, Reconciliation, Onboarding, ImportTemplate, Report, JournalDraft, TenantIsolationSweep, TripListMigration, SettlementUpgrade, Security (route summaries) — **95 tests, all passed** in their final runs (the two failures of the first run were the intended behaviour changes above, fixed in the tests). ## Owner findings of 27 Oct 2026 — budget layout, RPT-19 / RPT-20, sign-in texts (one commit; DT-268…DT-270 accepted 27 Oct)
1. **Budget screen layout (DT-268).** *Cause:* the top card's form still had the list-screen class `fill` (`flex: 1; min-height: 0; overflow: auto`) — the same mistake as DT-247 — so beside the grid it got a thin strip with its own scrollbar (Name cut off), and with an empty filter result it took all the free height (the big gap above the filters); the filter row was aligned at the bottom, so the cost centre's help text pushed its field up. *Fix:* the form flows at its full height (`.flow`, DT-247); the grid card takes the height left under the cards (at least 360 px — below that the page, the only page scrollbar, scrolls to it) and keeps its own sticky header, first column and totals; an empty result shows its message in that card right under the filters; the filters (account type, cost centre, search) line up at the top, the help text stays under its field without moving the others. UI test `Budget_layout_top_card_flows_filters_align_and_an_empty_result_sits_in_the_grid_card`.
2. **RPT-19 "No results" (DT-269).** *Cause:* RPT-19 listed only **open** items — trips and advances not yet in a settlement. In the demo company every driver's trips and advances were settled, so nothing was open; and the 100 on 1104 that RPT-23 shows is a **manual journal entry** naming driver 4 (an opening advance), which neither RPT-19 nor the driver statement RPT-13 read (both are built from documents). *Fix:* the driver and partner accounts include manual entries (with their reversals) and the opening entry that name the driver / partner (credit = owed to the party, debit = owed by it) — statements RPT-13 / RPT-14 (new line kind "Journal", opening the entry), the driver / partner account screens, and the balances; **RPT-19** now lists every driver with something on the account up to the date, also when settled to zero, with Trips, Wages, Advances, Settled (deductions and net paid, less repaid), Manual and opening entries and **Balance = the RPT-13 closing balance** — both come from one service (`AccountStatementService.DriverBalancesAsync`). **RPT-20** had the same kind of problem and one more: a partner's repayment was taken off the balance instead of added (−138 instead of −38 in the test); it now has Cost, Payments, Repaid by the partner, Manual and opening entries and Balance = the RPT-14 closing balance. The driver / partner accounts pages and the accounting dashboard read these reports (the balance stays the last column). Demo company after the fix: drivers 2 and 3 → 0 (all settled), driver 4 → −100 (the opening advance). API test `Driver_and_partner_balances_equal_the_statements_closing_balance` (drivers with only trips, only an advance, trips and an advance, all settled, only a manual entry; a partner paid, the trip voided after payment, a repayment and a manual entry) — every balance equals the statement's closing balance.
3. **Sign-in security and two-step texts (DT-270).** Tenant → Companies → Sign-in security: "These settings apply to every user of the organisation." / «تنطبق هذه الإعدادات على كل مستخدمي المنشأة.» (was "… Two-step verification is always required."); "Require two-step verification for all users": "When on, every user without two-step verification must set it up at their next sign-in." / «عند التفعيل، يجب على كل مستخدم ليس لديه تحقق بخطوتين أن يُعدّه عند دخوله التالي.» (no platform staff). Also outdated since revision 6: help "Sign-up and first-time setup" (two-step is offered at the first sign-in — recommended, required when the organisation turns it on — with "Skip for now"), help "My profile" (no "nor by platform staff" / "or you are platform staff"), the code page's note `acct.mandatory` ("…You manage it in My profile; if you lose your phone, use a recovery code or ask a user administrator to reset it." — it said you cannot turn it off yourself), the required set-up's note `acct.setupNote` ("Two-step verification is required for your account, so it cannot be turned off…"), the accepted invitation `acct.inviteDone` ("Sign in; you will be offered two-step verification (recommended)."). ## Owner findings of 27 Oct 2026, part 2 — paid partner trips, trip columns, pins (one commit; DT-271…DT-275 accepted 27 Oct)
Rules (owner): no E2E; API, UI (events through `UiTestBase.Act`) and one focused visual check; never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`.
1. **Finding A — cause (confirmed).** "Unsettled" was `SettlementId IS NULL`: "not in a driver settlement". Driver settlements only ever take trips **without** a partner (`SettlementService`: `PartnerId == null`), so every partner trip was "unsettled" forever, paid or not. *Demo company* (company 4, partner 2): 7 partner trips, one voided (54184); the 6 active ones cost 475.475 together; payments PPY-00001 233.475 (12 Oct) and PPY-00002 242.000 (19 Oct) = 475.475. Oldest first, PPY-00001 covers exactly 9496 (60.500), DEMO-003 (87.725) and DEMO-006 (85.250); PPY-00002 covers 16435 (79.750), 49892361 (82.500) and 467467 (79.750). All six are paid; "invoiced" (finding A's wording) is the customer side and unrelated.
2. **"Settled" = paid, for every trip (DT-271).** Driver trips: in a driver settlement (unchanged). Partner trips: the partner's net payments (active payments − repayments, the DT-230 rule) laid over its active trips oldest first (trip date, receipt number, id); fully covered = paid ("Paid to partner"), partly covered = unpaid with the open amount ("Partly paid"), voided = never paid. Deductions need no extra rule (the voided trip leaves the pool, newer trips absorb what was paid; the next payment adds only its money), repayments reduce the pool; the open parts of a partner's trips always add up to its balance due (tested). *Server:* views `trn.TRN_PartnerPaymentBoundary` (per partner: net paid, the first day not fully covered, cost before it) and `trn.TRN_TripPartnerCoverage` (per trip: open amount) + index `IX_TRN_Trips_PartnerOrder` (database revision 6 §B13, migration `Rev6_PartnerTripCoverage`). The trip list (filter, totals, paging, Excel/CSV/PDF) reads the boundaries once per request (`TripService.PaymentCuts`) and filters with plain conditions on the trip date — a running total over every trip measured 0.7–1.1 s on this PC and 12 s under the paged plan, so it is not used by the list; the partner filter now uses the partner's id (index seek). `TripResponse.Payment` (Settled / Unsettled / PartnerPaid / PartnerPartlyPaid / PartnerUnpaid) and `PartnerOpen`; `IsSettled` = paid. The report builder's Trips source: "Settled / paid" uses the same rule, new money field "Open to partner".
3. **Badges and trip view (DT-272).** Status column: "Settled" (driver trip), "Paid to partner", "Partly paid · x open" (AR/EN, status tokens, light and dark). Trip view: a line "Settled with the driver in settlement STL-…" with "Open settlement STL-…", or "Paid to the partner …" / "Partly paid to the partner …: x of y still open" / "Not yet paid to the partner …" with "Partner statement" (opens the partner account on its statement tab, `?tab=statement`). Partner account → Trips: new "Settlement / payment" column. Checked: "Partner trips" + "Unsettled" (the open partner trips only), exports, driver account (open trips = driver trips without a settlement — unchanged and right), partner account, RPT-19 / RPT-20 (balances, unchanged), dashboard (no card counts unsettled trips), report builder (fixed as above).
4. **Trips list columns (DT-273).** Columns chooser: Partner (shown by default, after Customer), Fleet type (own fleet / external / partner), Invoice no. (link to the invoice), Settlement / payment (all five states), Notes — the last four hidden until chosen; order, "Save for me", "Save as company default" as before; sort by Partner and Invoice no.; the filters already cover partner, partner trips, fleet and settled. The chooser says "Showing the usual columns; tick others to show them." when no view is saved. Exports: Excel/CSV "Fleet type" (words, was the code), "Invoice no.", "Settlement / payment", "Open to partner"; the PDF adds Partner, Invoice no. and Settlement / payment. A view saved before this change keeps its own choice (Partner is not added to it; none existed in the development database).
5. **Other list screens (DT-274).** Invoices, receipts, credit notes, settlements, advances and partner payments already show the party and the document number (receipts: the invoice; advances: the settlement) — nothing added. Possible extras, not added: settlements — payment method / repaid; partner payments — the deducted amount (DT-230); column choosers exist on the trips list only (DT-160).
6. **Pin a saved report (DT-275).** *Cause:* the dialog listed only the definition's summary measures; a report in detail mode has none, so "Measure shown" was empty; the "empty box" under it is most likely the browser's own list of a drop-down without options (not reproduced on the old version; the list can no longer be empty); the server also refused detail reports (`report.measure`) and labelled pins with raw keys ("sum revenue"). *Now:* `GET /reports/saved/{report}/pin-measures` lists "Number of rows" (key `rows`) plus every numeric total — a summary report's measures (by index; a plain count is the number of rows already) or a detail report's numeric columns (`sum:<field>`, "Sum of Revenue" / «مجموع الإيراد») — with current values; the dialog shows them (loading state; never empty), "Warn when: the value reaches or goes above / below the threshold", both thresholds optional with help, numbers only (the field's own message), the critical threshold at or beyond the warning one in the chosen direction (on screen and on the server: `report.thresholds` / `report.thresholdsBelow` on the critical field), and a preview of the dashboard card (value in its kind, state in the chosen direction). The dashboard card is the same component (`Pages/Reports/PinCard.razor`): counts without decimals, the bar only when warning above, "Warning: below x" / "Below the limit x". `cfg.DashboardPins.WarnWhen` (§B14), existing pins "Above" and keep their index. Every data source of the builder offers "Number of rows" and its totals (tested source by source).
7. **Tests.** API new `PaidTripsTests` (6: own-fleet settled/unsettled; partner trips paid oldest first — full, partly, unpaid, an older trip entered later, "Partner trips" + "Unsettled", totals = listed rows, paging, CSV/PDF export, report-builder field; paid trip voided with Deduct, newer trips, payment voided; voided with Return and the partner's repayment; pin measures for every data source; pinned value = report total, direction, refusals, old pins, dashboard, unpin) — pass; affected classes (Trip, Report, Cash, PaidPartnerTrip, SettlementRepayment, Invoice, AccountingDashboard; isolation sweep, security, migration and upgrade tests, Reconciliation, MasterData, Collaboration, FieldMessage) **98 passed**. Performance check (explicit, 500,000 trips, half of them partner trips) **passed**: default list 323 ms, this month 156 ms, flags 374 ms, search 236 ms, Unsettled 526 ms, Partner trips + Unsettled 378 ms, a partner's paid trips 666 ms; the split is exact (100,000 paid / 150,000 open). *Found on the way:* since DT-231 the API test factory replaced the database name, so `tools/perf/run-performance-check.*` ran on the shared `DirectServiceMaster_ApiTests` instead of `DirectServiceMaster_PerfTests`; the factory now keeps the database named by `DST_API_TEST_DB` (subclasses still use their own). Application **473 passed**, UI **333 passed** (new `PaidTripsPageTests`, 7: badges AR/EN, Partner column shown / moved / saved with the optional columns AR/EN, pin dialog lists and previews AR/EN, thresholds must be numbers and in order), Infrastructure **68 passed** (audit words for `WarnWhen`). **Visual** (`VISUAL_ONLY="paid27#"`, 1366, AR/EN, light/dark, `timeout` + `--global-timeout=600000`): trips list, Columns chooser, list with the optional columns, Partner trips + Unsettled, partly paid trip view, pin dialog, dashboard card — 2 passed, no layout findings; checked by eye. Not done: the OpenAPI document was not exported again (new route `pin-measures`, new fields).

## Owner findings of 27 Oct 2026, part 3 — previous balances, dashboard sides, cancelled journal entries (one commit; DT-276…DT-280 accepted 27 Oct)
Rules (owner): no E2E; API, UI (events through `UiTestBase.Act`) and one focused visual check; never push; never commit `appsettings.json` or `docs/feedback`. Earlier work kept: DT-226 / DT-228 repayments and carried rest, DT-230 partner rules, DT-269 balances, DT-271…DT-275.
1. **Finding A — cause.** Since DT-269 the driver's balance includes manual / opening entries (JV-00042: 100 on 1104 naming سامي العبادي → −100), but settlements only took open trips and advances, so the 100 could never be deducted or collected ("Nothing open to settle"); the accounting dashboard summed the signed RPT-19 / RPT-20 balances into "What we owe" (Drivers −100).
2. **Driver settlements settle the previous balance (DT-276).** The settlement (preview, form, view, printout) shows "Previous balance (manual / opening entries)" as its own line with the entries (each opens the journal entry) and "Settle it with this settlement" (ticked by default). Net = wages − advances − deductions + previous balance: owed by the driver → deducted like an advance, a shortfall repaid by cash / bank / CliQ or carried to the next settlement as an open advance (DT-228 rules); owed to the driver → added to the net. A settlement may have no trips and no advances (only that balance). The entry clears the amounts **per journal entry and account with the driver** (`PreviousBalances.Lines`: Dr what was owed to the driver, Cr what the driver owed — e.g. Cr 1104 100 with the driver), stored in `trn.TRN_PreviousBalanceItems` (`Settlement.PreviousBalance` = their total). Voiding the settlement makes them open again (items of a void document do not count). Only entries in force count (a voided / edited entry and its reversal cancel out). Driver account: its own figure and tab "Previous balance" (opens first when it is the only thing open), "Driver owes the company" instead of a negative net, "Nothing open to settle" only when trips, advances and that balance are all zero (else "No open trips"). Help page "Driver advance and settlement" updated (AR/EN).
3. **Partners (DT-277).** The partner account has the same figure / tab; the next payment settles the previous balance first — owed by the partner: set off against what its trips are owed (a payment may then be 0, e.g. 25 owed and trips of 176 → pay 151); owed to the partner: paid first from the payment; or a repayment repays what the partner owes (in parts, oldest entry first). "Settle it with this payment" can be unticked. Partner payable takes only the **trip part** (payment: amount − previous balance; repayment: amount + previous balance), the previous balance is cleared on its own accounts with the partner. *DT-271 interaction:* trip coverage ("Paid to partner") counts only the trip part of payments and repayments (view `trn.TRN_PartnerPaymentBoundary` changed, `PartnerPaymentService.TripPart`; also the DT-230 paid-trip check and the partner-shipments report). So a 151 cash payment that sets off 25 the partner owes pays 176 of trips (both trips "Paid to partner"); a 191 payment with 15 owed to the partner pays 176 of trips; a manual balance never makes trips look paid or unpaid on its own. "Paid" on the partner account = paid for trips.
4. **Reports stay at 0 after settling.** RPT-13 / RPT-14 (statement closing), RPT-19 / RPT-20 and RPT-23: the reconciliation nets what documents settled with the manual / opening lines (no "unexplained" gap, the settled entry is no longer listed) and leaves those clearing lines out of "what documents posted"; the partner-payable rebuild uses the trip part (which also fixes repayments being counted as payments there).
5. **Dashboard and lists without negatives (DT-278).** Accounting dashboard: top cards "We owe suppliers, drivers and partners" and "Drivers, partners and suppliers owe us"; boxes "What we owe (we must pay)" and "Owed to us (they owe the company)" (with a help line), each line linking to the list filtered to its side (`driver-accounts?side=owed|owes`, `partner-accounts?side=owed|owes`, payables). Figures from `AccountStatementService` balances split by sign (a supplier paid more than billed counts as owing the company). RPT-19 / RPT-20 (= Driver accounts / Partner accounts lists): the balance column is now two non-negative columns "Owed to the driver / partner" and "Owes the company" (statement closing = first − second) with totals; `side=owed|owes` filter (Show: All / Owed to … / Owes the company on both list screens, from the address too). Main dashboard checked: it shows the month's wages and partner costs only — no balances, nothing negative.
6. **Journal form note (DT-280).** A line on a driver or partner control account (advances / payable) shows "For driver advances, use Driver accounts > Advance so they appear in settlements." / «لسلف السائقين استخدم حسابات السائقين ← سلفة حتى تظهر في التسويات.» (partners: "For partner payments, use Partner accounts > Payment so they count for the partner's trips."); never blocks.
7. **Finding B — journal tabs (DT-279).** *What they contained:* "Void" = entries with status Void — only manual entries voided on the journal (FR-113); "Reversed" = entries reversed because their document was voided or changed (trips, invoices, receipts, returns, advances, settlements, partner payments, AP/AR documents), a manual entry edited (reverse + re-post, D-05) or the opening balances saved again; the cancelling entries are Posted and appeared under All / Manual / Automatic as "Reverses JV-…". Discarded drafts are deleted, never listed. *Development database:* no manual entry had been voided (JV-00042/55/59/60 are all posted; the audit log shows voids of receipts RCV-9/10, invoices 14/15, returns RTN-1/2 and a trip), so "Void" was empty and every cancelled entry was under "Reversed" — manual voids were not missing; voiding a manual entry works and is listed (tested). *Now:* one tab "Cancelled" («الملغاة») with a help text, listing every cancelled entry (Reversed or Void, API `status=Cancelled`; old `?view=Reversed|Void` addresses open it) with "Cancelled by JV-…" and a "Cancel reason" column (a voided manual entry: its reason; an edit: "Entry edited: a corrected entry replaced it"; the opening entry: "Opening balances saved again"; a document: "Document voided: <its reason>" or "Document changed and posted again"); the cancelling entries are "Reversal of JV-…" under All and Manual / Automatic; the entry view links both ways and its banner gives the reason; the status tag "Reversed" reads "Cancelled by reversal" («أُلغي بقيد عكسي»). Filters, search, counts and Excel / CSV / PDF exports follow the same rule (exports gained "Cancellation" and "Cancel reason" columns).
8. **Found on the way.** The settlement's trips header lost its style since DT-234 wrapped table cards in `.box-x` ("…27/10/20260 selected"); fixed in `app.css`. `database/MasterDatabase_Idempotent.sql` regenerated (it also lacked `Rev6_PartnerTripCoverage`). OpenAPI exported again (521 operations, incl. the DT-275 `pin-measures` route), Redocly 2.62.1 lint valid.
9. **Database:** migration `Rev6_PreviousBalances` (revision 6 §B15): `TRN_Settlements.PreviousBalance`, `TRN_PartnerPayments.PreviousBalance`, table `trn.TRN_PreviousBalanceItems`, checks, view change; rollback refused once used (THROW 50276). Applied to the development database.
10. **Tests.** API new `PreviousBalanceTests` (6: driver with only a manual balance owing the company — preview, `settlement.empty` when left out, repayment, entry Cr 1104 with the driver, RPT-13 / RPT-19 / RPT-23 at 0, void restores; owed by the company paid without trips; trips + balance deducted; more than the wages → repaid 20 and 30 carried with RPT-23 still matching; partner set-off with both trips "Paid to partner", refusal above cost − 25, RPT-14 / RPT-20 / RPT-23 at 0, void restores, a 0 payment sets off 25 → oldest trip partly paid; partner repayments in parts and a balance owed to the partner paid first; dashboard split and `side`; Cancelled view with voided manual entry, edit and voided advance, manual / automatic split, search, links both ways, CSV export) and the affected classes (Report, Cash, PaidTrips, PaidPartnerTrip, SettlementRepayment, AccountingDashboard, Reconciliation, LedgerReport, Accounting, JournalDraft, JournalTax, Trip; SettlementUpgrade, TripListMigration, JournalTaxUpgrade, TenantIsolationSweep, Security, Collaboration, MasterData, FieldMessage, Invoice, ApAr, ReleaseRegression, BudgetProfitLoss) — **171 passed**. Application **473 passed**, Infrastructure **68 passed**, UI **344 passed** (new `PreviousBalancePageTests`, 11: settlement line AR/EN with untick and the request, driver account tab / figure / "Nothing open", dashboard boxes and links AR/EN, driver list side from the address, journal Cancelled view AR/EN, old `?view=Void`, form note for driver / partner). **Visual** (`VISUAL_ONLY="pb27#"`, 1366, AR/EN, light/dark, `timeout` + `--global-timeout=600000`): driver account, settlement with the previous-balance line, accounting dashboard, journal Cancelled — 2 passed, no layout findings; checked by eye (it found the duplicate voided-entry lines and the trips header, both fixed and re-checked).

## Platform restructuring — Stage 1: design (27 Oct 2026; documents only, no code / project / migration / configuration change)
Owner decisions behind it: "Direct Service" platform with products (Transportation first; Accounting shared and subscribable alone; next system undecided, maybe POS); one application, one sign-in, one database, one provider console; per-product free-trial offers turned on/off by platform staff; manual activation / renewal / extension / plan change until the payment gateway; code rename `DirectService.Transportation.*` → `DirectService.*` in later stages; tax codes to Accounting > Setup & control; chart template 4103 / 4105 "Other income" (5206 stays Operating).
1. **`docs/platform/01_Restructuring_Design.md`** — placement of every table, entity, service, endpoint group, worker, notification type, permission, page, report, help topic and test class (Shared / Platform / Accounting / Transportation, unclear items U-1…U-11 with answers); shared parties (`acc.ApArParties` as the shared register, transport customers as linked profiles, drivers/partners via party providers); the accounting posting contract (`IAccountingPosting`, handlers, mapping keys per product, same transaction, reversal, idempotency; transport-only accounting features hidden for Accounting-only tenants); **one composed `MasterDbContext`** with module contributors, same migration history and ids, no migration in Stages 2–4; the **names stored in data** inventory E-1…E-37 with the pin for each; products / subscriptions / trials (database revision 7: six new `core` tables, existing tenants get TRN + ACC unchanged); UI composition; rename plan; tests and CI; Stages 2–5 with scope, proofs, estimates (≈ 240–320 h), risks, rollback; owner decisions K-01…K-23.
2. **`docs/platform/02_How_To_Add_A_New_System.md`** (draft, finalised in Stage 5) and **`docs/platform/00_START_HERE.md`** (draft, finalised after Stage 5).
3. **Main risks found:** the audit entity name is computed from namespace + class (`TRN.Trip`) and feeds `core.AuditLogs`, live refresh, record locks and history text; URL-token keys use the class name (`DST-URL:Trip`) — bookmarks, notification links, saved-report filters, emailed links; the development Data Protection key ring sits in the Web project folder (`.local/keys`); `EmailOutbox.Template` uses `nameof`; the Blazor `…Web.Client.lib.module.js` is named after the assembly; assembly scans in `EntityResolver`, `AuditDescriber` and the tenant deletion. All are pinned in Stage 2, step 0, before any file moves, with golden tests.
4. **Reviewed by the owner** (26/27 Oct 2026); decisions recorded 28 Oct 2026 (next section). Stage 2 started 28 Oct 2026.

## Platform restructuring — Stage 2, session 1: step 0, names stored in data pinned (28 Oct 2026; DT-281…DT-286, for owner review)
No file, project or namespace moved; behaviour, routes, database, OpenAPI and every stored value unchanged (proofs in item 4). Local commit only, no E2E, no visual check.
1. **Pins** (design §e; the new table e.4 says where each one lives).
   - `[StoredName("TRN.Trip")]` and `[TokenLabel("Trip")]` on all **78 entities**; `Domain/Common/StoredNames.cs` reads them, and a missing pin throws (never a fallback to the code name).
   - Switched to the pins: the audit interceptor (`EntityTypeName` → audit log, live refresh, append-only messages), `EntityResolver` (record locks), `AuditDescriber`, `UrlTokenService` (HKDF info `DST-URL:` + label), `CompanyExportService.RecordType`, tenant deletion. The assembly scans are replaced by `Application/Abstractions/EntityTypeMap.cs` (built from the EF model).
   - Email template names: `EmailTemplateNames` (14 constants; `nameof` removed).

   - Already constants, only documented and golden-tested: permission keys and preset roles, setting keys, document types, posting keys, accounting codes, notification types, field-catalog types, source and audit entity-type constants, auth schemes and claims, token providers, user-secrets ids.
   - **Count per kind:** 78 entity stored names + 78 token labels; 14 email template names; 4 Data Protection purposes + application name + key folder; 3 cookies + 1 header + 2 schemes + 3 claims + 3 token-provider names; SignalR 1 path + 1 group prefix + 2 methods + 4 events; 3 localStorage parts (+ 2 JS literals checked); 4 file / resource paths. Existing constant sets under golden test: 26 permission keys + 5 preset roles, 13 setting constants (keys, prefix, patterns), 12 document types, 14 posting keys, 14 accounting codes + 2 CHECK lists, 8 notification types, 3 field-catalog types, 26 audit entity-type and 9 source-type constants, 10 report sources with their field keys.
   - **Found not stored / not a key (left as they are):** E-7 (migration metadata), E-17 (pins hold no type names), E-30 (configuration names), E-35 (launch profile), the company-export ZIP entry names, the payment gateway's `dst:` application-lock name, exception type names in e-mail error texts. E-6 (audit JSON keys = property names) is guarded by the golden property lists, not pinned.
2. **Golden tests.** New project `tests/DirectService.Architecture.Tests` (in the solution; first test step in `ci.yml`), **22 tests**:
   - entities: every entity has both pins and none is shared; stored names through the interceptor; URL tokens (fixed key + GUID = the tag's 22-character token for every type); entity resolver, audit history, company export and tenant-deletion type sets; entity property lists; the browser's stored-name literals are golden names;

   - the OpenAPI document (449 paths, 521 operations, 409 schemas, SHA-256).

A second run (to add the accounting codes and field-catalog types) gave byte-identical files for everything else.
3. **The tests catch a change** (experiments, not committed, undone): `Announcement` moved to another namespace with the name computed as at the tag → fails "missing now: CORE.Announcement / new: PLATFORM.Announcement"; the same move with the pins → all green; `Trip` without its pins → 7 tests fail "…Trip has no [StoredName]"; `Trip`'s token label changed to follow a new class name → fails "TRN.Trip: token label TripRecord, golden Trip … every saved link of this type would break".

## GitHub CI failure of 28 Oct 2026 — Architecture tests step (one commit; DT-287, for owner review)
- **Symptom:** step "Architecture tests (stored names, golden files)": "Zero tests ran", exit code 5, "Handshake failures".
- **Cause:** not the coverage extension. When session 1 added the step, the scripted edit wrote a literal `\n` instead of a line break, so the committed command was one line `… --no-build \n --coverage …`; bash turns `\n` into a stray argument `n`, which the test host refuses as an invalid command line (exit code 5). Reproduced locally with the command exactly as committed (same output). The project itself already had the shared set-up of `tests/Directory.Build.props` (identical generated extension registration and coverage assets as the other projects).
- **Fix:** the step's line continuation restored. `tests/Directory.Build.props` now applies the test set-up to every project whose name ends in `.Tests` (any new test project gets it automatically); other projects under `tests/` (the coming helper library `DirectService.Testing`) stay plain libraries that no test command runs. New `CiWorkflowTests` in Architecture.Tests: no workflow command line carries a stray backslash sequence, every `dotnet test` line has exactly the CI options (Release, `--no-build`, the three coverage options), and every `*.Tests` project under `tests/` is run by `ci.yml` (loops expanded). Shown to fail on the broken workflow.
- **Proof (the `ci.yml` commands locally: restore, `build --no-restore -c Release`, then each test command with `--coverage`):** Architecture 24/24, Domain 4/4, Application 473/473, UI 344/344, Infrastructure 68/68, Api 315 passed + 2 skipped (four parts by class with the same arguments); each wrote its `TestResults/<name>.cobertura.xml`. Not checked: the run on the GitHub runner itself (the owner's next push).

No route, API, database, OpenAPI, stored value, sign-in or screen changed; golden files not regenerated. Local commit only, no E2E, no visual check.

No route, API, database object, OpenAPI, stored value, sign-in or screen changed; migration ids and history unchanged; golden files not regenerated. Local commit only, no E2E, no visual check.

2. **Composed context** (`src/Shared/DirectService.Shared.Infrastructure/Persistence`): `MasterDbContext` (same class name, `IAppDbContext`, collation, no automatic FK indexes, `DF_<Table>_<Column>` names, the named query filters `SoftDelete` / `Tenant` / `Company` for every entity with the scope interfaces) now takes `IEnumerable<IModelContributor>` and refuses to build a model with none; `QueryFilters`; `MasterDatabase.UseMasterDatabase` (SQL Server + `MigrationsAssembly("DirectService.Database")` + `MigrationsHistoryTable("__EFMigrationsHistory")`) used by the host, the factory and every test context. `TransportationModelContributor` (in Infrastructure, where the configurations still are) applies all configurations of its assembly, the reference data seed and the report SQL functions — the same calls in the same order as the old `OnModelCreating`.

0. **Commit guard (DT-328, commit 65bba0b):** `.githooks/pre-commit` (kept LF by `.gitattributes`), enabled for this clone with `git config core.hooksPath .githooks` and documented for every new clone in `docs/step9/Setup_and_Run.md` §1. It refuses `src/DirectService.Transportation.Web/appsettings.json`, anything under `docs/feedback/` and any other `appsettings*.json` that sets a key / secret / password / token (or a connection string with a password) to a non-empty value, naming the line numbers but never the value; Visual Studio and the command line both run it. Proved: the owner's `appsettings.json`, a `docs/feedback` screenshot and a throw-away settings file with a secret were each refused (exit 1, the message shown); the guard's own commit and every later commit went through. Files are staged by explicit path and `git diff --cached --name-only` is checked before each commit.
1. **Audit engine → Shared (step 5, DT-329, commit 64278ef):** `AuditDescriber` (same name) → `Shared.Infrastructure/Audit/`, registered as `IAuditDescriber`; `AuditRow`, `AuditDescription`, `DescribeOptions`, `ParsedAuditRow`, `IAuditRun`, `AuditActionDetail`, `AuditLines` and `IAuditVocabularyProvider` in SharedKernel `Audit/AuditDescriptions.cs`; `Words` and the vocabulary shapes (`AuditWords.EntityWords`, `ActionWords`, `FieldWords`, `Kind`, the unknown-type / unknown-action / sentence / humanize rules) in `Audit/AuditWords.cs`; `AuditVocabularies` combines the modules. `AuditVocabulary` stays in Transportation with every text unchanged (plus `PersonalFields`, the lists `ErasedRecords` held, which now takes them as a parameter). Every rule of the DT-327 list is a hook of `TransportationAuditVocabulary` with its literal values (journal entry and lines, rows named by their user / role, documents named by actions, `Approve.Support`, the draft-invoice wording, source titles, action extras, the "Statement " reason, drivers masked without drv.view, code texts, company-setting values, record names, `ACC.ApArDocument` link kinds, users read unfiltered and without a last-known name). Standard field labels through `FieldCatalogs`. `FieldChange`, `HistoryLine`, `HistoryLink` → Shared.Contracts (same names; OpenAPI identical). Callers (`AuditLogService`, `MasterDataSupport`, `BudgetService`) take `IAuditDescriber`.
2. **Report builder → Shared (step 6 part 1, DT-330, commit eed1a44):** `ReportBuilderService` (same name, with `GroupKey` / `GroupRow`) → `Shared.Infrastructure/Reports/`, registered as itself and `IReportBuilder`; `FieldTypes`, `FieldDef`, `SourceDef`, `IReportSourceProvider`, `IReportBuilder` and `ExportLimits` in SharedKernel `ReportBuilder.cs`; `SqlJson` → SharedKernel (still mapped by the Transportation model contributor, like `SqlText`). `ReportSources` stays in Transportation (source and field keys unchanged, E-16) and is served by `TransportationReportSources` (the ledger period defaults, the built-in ownership-type names, the journal entry behind a journal line). The result and definition records (`ResultColumn`, `DrillLink`, `ResultRow`, `ReportResult`, `ReportFilterValue`, `SourceField`, `DataSourceInfo`, `ReportGrouping`, `ReportMeasure`, `ReportFilter`, `ReportSort`, `ReportDefinition`, `ReportRunRequest`, `PinMeasureOption`, `ReportJson`) → Shared.Contracts (same names); the builder's 18 error codes → `SharedErrorCodes` (same values, aliases in `ErrorCodes`). The unused constant `ReportBuilderService.Permission` was removed (it named a Transportation permission). `SavedReportService`, `ExportService` and the report endpoints take `IReportBuilder`; saved reports and pins unchanged.
3. **Tests:** no count changed. Changed without changing an assertion: `AuditDescriberTests` (constructs the shared describer with `TransportationAuditVocabulary` and `FieldCatalogs`), API / UI tests that named the moved report records with their old namespace.
4. **Proof (final state):** `build -c Release` (whole solution): 0 warnings, 0 errors; `has-pending-model-changes`: no changes; throw-away `migrations add ThrowawayS9`: empty `Up` / `Down`, removed with `migrations remove` (snapshot unchanged); idempotent script byte-identical (356,050 bytes) to `database/MasterDatabase_Idempotent.sql`; host started once (Release, Development profile): `/openapi/v1.json` byte-identical (2,198,458 bytes) to `docs/api/openapi-v1.json`, host stopped (port 52233 free). Tests (the `ci.yml` commands, each project with `--coverage`, in foreground parts): Architecture 37/37, Domain 4/4, Application 473/473, UI 344/344, Infrastructure 69/69, Api 316 passed + 2 skipped (the explicit 500,000-trip checks) of 318 in five parts (A–B 54, C–I 78, J–P 92, Q–S 52, T–Z 42).
5. **Moved to the next session (DT-331):** step 6 part 2 (`SavedReportService` with the pins, its DTOs and the paging DTOs `PagedResult` / `PageRequest` / `Ref`, used across most of the code) and step 7 (exports, which take saved reports) — plan in "Stage 2, proposed sessions" (session 10).

## Platform restructuring — Stage 2, session 10: shared engines, part 5 (29 Oct 2026; DT-332…DT-335, for owner review)
No route, API, database object, OpenAPI, stored value, message, audit or history text, printed document, sign-in or screen changed; migration ids and history unchanged; golden files not regenerated; class and property names, pin values, keys, measure keys and `WarnWhen` values unchanged. 1. **Paging records → Shared.Contracts (step 1, DT-332, commit 78f00f2):** `PagedResult`, `PageRequest` and `Ref` (`Contracts/Common/Paging.cs`, the only file of that namespace) → `Shared.Contracts/Common/Paging.cs` (namespace `DirectService.Shared.Contracts`, members unchanged). Mechanical (97 files): 82 `using` / `@using` lines removed (every project concerned already has the global `using DirectService.Shared.Contracts`) and six references written as `Contracts.Common.X` now name `DirectService.Shared.Contracts.X`.

Its dependencies: sources from the modules' `IReportSourceProvider`s (same `CanUse` checks), tokens through `IUrlTokenService` (what `MasterDataSupport.Token` / `Decode` called), problem texts through `IErrorText`, the administrator permission through `SavedReportOptions` (`settings.manage`, set by Transportation), the search text through the new `TextNormalizer.SearchText` (`MasterDataSupport.SearchText` forwards to it), the export table through `ReportTable.FromResult` (`StandardReportService.ToTable` forwards to it). `AuditTrail` (only SharedKernel dependencies) → `SharedKernel/Audit/`, registered by `AddSharedInfrastructure`. `ReportName`, `ReportThresholds`, `ReportThresholdsBelow` and `ServerError` are defined in `SharedErrorCodes` (same values, aliases in `ErrorCodes`, messages unchanged). The saved-report and pin DTOs (`SavedReportRequest`, `SavedReportResponse`, `SavedReportListQuery`, `DashboardPinRequest`, `PinStates`, `DashboardPinResponse`, `PinOrderRequest`) → `Shared.Contracts/Reports/SavedReportContracts.cs`. `DashboardService` and the dashboard DTOs stay in Transportation.

Report-builder and saved-report exports run in the engine; the kinds `standard`, `audit-log` and `company` are `TransportationExportJobs` (same checks and files), `CORE.ExportReady` / `CORE.ExportFailed` are sent by `ExportNotifications` with the same parameters and link `/exports/{id}`. `ExportWorker` stays in the host.

Steps 1 and 2 were committed after the build, Architecture / Domain / Application / UI and (step 2) the report, collaboration and accounting API classes (23/23).

## Platform restructuring — Stage 2, session 11: Platform domain and infrastructure (29 Oct 2026; DT-336…DT-341, for owner review)
No route, API, database object, OpenAPI, stored value, message, audit or history text, printed document, e-mail, sign-in (cookies, 2FA, sessions, recovery codes, Data Protection purposes and key ring) or screen changed; migration ids and history unchanged; golden files not regenerated; class and property names and pin values unchanged. Local commits only (two step commits + this status commit), no E2E, no visual check.
1. **core.* entities and configurations → Platform (step 1, DT-336…DT-338, commit 14626ce):** the 18 core entities (all but `AuditLog`, Shared since session 8, and `MaintenanceLog`, which stays in `Transportation.Domain/Core` for Accounting, K-11) → `src/Platform/DirectService.Platform.Domain` (namespace `DirectService.Platform.Domain`; only the namespace line changed); their 18 configurations and `CrossModuleRelationships` (the foreign keys of the Shared tables to the anchors, same names) → `Platform.Infrastructure/Persistence`, applied by the new `PlatformModelContributor` (registered by `AddPlatformInfrastructure`, which the host calls after `AddInfrastructure`; `MasterModel.Contributors` = Shared, Transportation, Platform — the host's order). Every `using …Domain.Core` became `using DirectService.Platform.Domain` (files that use `MaintenanceLog` keep both).
   - **Seed split (DT-337):** `PermissionSeed` + `IPermissionSeedProvider` in `Platform.Contracts/Security` (browser-safe record); `PlatformReferenceDataSeed` holds the `core.*` rows (Ids 20–22) and the TRIAL plan; Transportation's `ReferenceDataSeed` gives its `trn.*` / `acc.*` rows (1–19, 23–26; the `acc.*` rows go to Accounting in Stage 3); the Platform contributor writes them all in one `HasData` ordered by Id (same values, Ids, PublicIds, `CreatedAt`) and refuses a duplicate Id or key. The moved Arabic texts were compared byte for byte with the former file.
   - **E-1 (DT-338):** `Transportation.Domain` references `Platform.Domain` for its `User` navigations (`VoidedByUser`, `ApprovedByUser`, `ClosedByUser`, `ExportedByUser`, `ChangedByUser`; the Tenant / Company / User navigations of `MaintenanceLog`). New test `LayerRuleTests.Modules_use_only_the_platform_anchor_types_for_foreign_keys_E1`: the type references of the compiled Transportation Domain and Infrastructure into `Platform.Domain` are `Tenant`, `Company` and `User` only (read from the assembly metadata); a Domain property of a Platform type must be a foreign-key navigation of the EF model; no Domain method or field uses a Platform type; Transportation.Infrastructure references no Platform project other than `Platform.Contracts`. `Domain_projects_reference_only_SharedKernel` allows `Platform.Domain` for a module's Domain (not for Platform.Domain itself).
2. **Identity, e-mail and billing infrastructure → Platform (step 2, DT-339, DT-340, commit b828404):** `UserStore`, `ReplaySafeAuthenticatorTokenProvider`, `StaffInvitationTokenProvider` (+ options) → `Platform.Infrastructure/Identity`; `OutboxEmailSender`, `EmailDispatcher`, `EmailDispatchState`, `EmailOutboxSignal`, `DataProtectionEmailProtector`, `FileEmailTransport`, `GraphEmailTransport`, `MimeMessageBuilder` and `EmailLogo` with the logo resource (same LogicalName `DST.Email.Logo.png`) → `Platform.Infrastructure/Email`; `FakePaymentGateway`, `SqlPlatformLock`, `PlatformNumbers` → `Platform.Infrastructure/Billing` (`IPaymentGateway` unchanged). Their registrations (Identity options, token providers, 24-hour / 48-hour token lifetimes, `PlatformOptions`, `Email` options, transports, Graph HTTP client) moved unchanged into `AddPlatformInfrastructure`. `DataProtectionPurposes` → `Shared.Infrastructure/Security` (values unchanged; used by the Platform classes and `EInvoiceStore`). `Azure.Identity` moved with the Graph transport. Temporary reference `Platform.Infrastructure` → `Transportation.Application` (the services these classes serve are still there), pinned by the new test `Platform_infrastructure_references_its_application_and_shared_infrastructure`; it goes in session 12.
3. **Tests:** Architecture 37 → 39 (E-1 anchors; Platform.Infrastructure references); no count dropped. Changed without changing an assertion: the E-32 golden test reads the resources of the logo's assembly (`typeof(EmailLogo).Assembly`, the same single resource), `FoundationTests.Every_permission_group_of_the_seed_has_its_heading_in_both_languages` reads both seed files (still 9 groups), tests that named moved types by their old namespace.

## Platform restructuring — Stage 2, proposed sessions (2–4 h each; owner runs them one by one)
Every session: no route, database, OpenAPI or stored-value change; ends with a local commit, all .NET tests green (foreground, time limits), Architecture.Tests green, `has-pending-model-changes` clean, idempotent script identical, a short report and a DT entry. Namespaces may change from session 2 on (the pins keep every stored name); class and property names do not.
- **Session 2 — project skeleton + SharedKernel + Testing.** **Done 28 Oct 2026** (section above). Create the empty projects in their folders (`src/Shared/DirectService.SharedKernel`, `.Shared.Infrastructure`, `.Shared.UI`; `src/Platform/DirectService.Platform.{Domain,Application,Infrastructure,Contracts,UI}`; `src/Hosts/DirectService.Database`; `tests/DirectService.Testing`) with their references (a.2); move to SharedKernel: `AuditableEntity`, scope interfaces, `StoredNames` + attributes, `IAppDbContext`, `IRequestContext`, `IDataScope`, `AppException`, `TextNormalizer`, `Money`; move `DatabaseFixture` / `ApiFactory` helpers to `DirectService.Testing`. First layer-rule tests (Domain → SharedKernel only; Shared references no Platform/module).
- **Session 3 — Database project + composed context.** **Done 28 Oct 2026** (section above). Migrations (20 + snapshot) and `DesignTimeDbContextFactory` to `DirectService.Database` (ids unchanged, `MigrationsAssembly` and `MigrationsHistoryTable("__EFMigrationsHistory","dbo")` explicit, `UserSecretsId` `dst-infrastructure-designtime` kept); `MasterDbContext` (same name), interceptors and query filters to Shared.Infrastructure with `IModelContributor`s; a throw-away `migrations add` must be empty.
- **Session 4 — Shared building blocks.** **Done in part 28 Oct 2026** (section above; the rest moved to session 5). `LiveChangeInterceptor` → Shared.Infrastructure together with URL tokens (from session 3, DT-296; it also needs `IRealtimeNotifier` / `IAfterCommit` behind Shared abstractions); cfg.* entities and configurations (except `EInvoiceSettings`), numbering, record locks, URL tokens, audit engine (vocabulary stays per module later), files, printing, spreadsheets, exports, search engine, report-builder engine, custom / standard fields, column views, import batches → Shared.Infrastructure; snapshot CLR strings updated mechanically for moved types.
- **Session 5 — cfg entities.** **Part 1 done 28 Oct 2026** (the five cfg entities, section above); the rest went to session 6.
- **Session 6 — Shared engines, part 1.** **Done 28 Oct 2026** (section above): `Shared.Contracts`, shared error codes, `Fail` / `Validator`, spreadsheets, column views, record locks, global search.
- **Session 7 — Shared engines, part 2.** **Done in part 28 Oct 2026** (section above: error texts, imports, fields; the rest went to session 8). Planned: First an error-text abstraction in SharedKernel (`IErrorText.Format(code, language, args)` + the import fix text), implemented over `ErrorCatalog` / `ImportFixCatalog`, so Shared code can produce messages without the Contracts; then, each with its DTOs to `Shared.Contracts` and the consumer-facing part as an interface or helper in SharedKernel (Transportation services call them directly today):
  - imports: `ImportProblems` / `ImportParse` (`Application/Accounting/ImportSupport.cs`) → SharedKernel; `ImportPreview` / `ImportRowResult` / `ImportProblem` / `ImportResult` → Shared.Contracts; `ImportTemplateService` engine + `IImportTemplateProvider`;
  - custom / standard fields: `FieldCatalog` content → `IFieldCatalogProvider` (TRN.Trip / Driver / Customer, values unchanged), `FieldRules` + an `IFieldRules` / `ICustomFieldValues` interface in SharedKernel, the services to Shared.Infrastructure ("field has data" checks through the provider);
  - audit engine: `AuditDescriber` → Shared.Infrastructure with `IAuditVocabularyProvider` (today's `AuditVocabulary` as the provider);
  - report builder: `ReportBuilderService`, `SavedReportService`, `ReportExporter` (paging DTOs `PagedResult` / `PageRequest` / `Ref` → Shared.Contracts) with `IReportSourceProvider`;
  - exports: `ExportService` + `ChannelExportQueue`.
  If the room runs out, the remaining engines move to session 8 and the later sessions move up by one.
- **Session 8 — Shared engines, part 3.** **Done in part 29 Oct 2026** (section above: steps 1–4; steps 5–7 went to session 9). Planned, in this order, each step a green commit:
  1. Platform rows behind SharedKernel interfaces, implemented in Transportation for now (Platform in session 9): `ICompanyInfo` (name, logo, address, tax number, currency decimals, time zone of the current company — what the print header, the exporter and the audit text read) and user names in batches (extend `IUserNames`).
  2. `AuditLog` → SharedKernel with its configuration (`SharedModelContributor`; foreign keys to Tenants / Companies / Users in `CrossModuleRelationships`, same names); pins and properties unchanged; throw-away migration empty.
  3. Printing helpers (`DocumentPrinter`, `AmountInWords`, `BrandFonts`, `PrintMode`, `IssuedNames`, `ErasedRecords`) → Shared; the embedded fonts move with `BrandFonts` with the same LogicalNames `fonts/<file>` (E-32 golden test follows the assembly, values unchanged).
  4. `ReportExporter` (+ `ReportTable`, `ReportColumn`, `ReportValue`) → Shared.Infrastructure behind an `IReportExporter` interface in SharedKernel (about 20 Transportation services call it).
  5. Audit engine: `AuditDescriber` → Shared.Infrastructure with `IAuditVocabularyProvider` (today's `AuditVocabulary` plus the module rules now inside the describer: driver personal data masked without drv.view, posting-key names, journal lines, document kinds).
  6. Report builder: `ReportBuilderService`, `SavedReportService` with `IReportSourceProvider` (`ReportSources` as the provider); paging DTOs (`PagedResult`, `PageRequest`, `Ref`) and report DTOs → Shared.Contracts.
  7. Exports: `ExportService` + `ChannelExportQueue`.
- **Session 9 — Shared engines, part 4.** **Done in part 29 Oct 2026** (section above: commit guard, step 1 and the report builder of step 2; saved reports and exports → session 10). Planned, steps 5–7 of session 8, in this order, each a green commit with the full proof at the end:
  1. Audit engine: `AuditDescriber` (with `AuditRow`, `AuditDescription`, `DescribeOptions`, `FieldChange`, `HistoryLine`, `HistoryLink`) → Shared.Infrastructure; `Words` and the vocabulary record types (`EntityWords`, `ActionWords`, `FieldWords`, the value kind) → SharedKernel; `IAuditVocabularyProvider` in SharedKernel, combined over the modules; today's `AuditVocabulary` (same class, every text unchanged) and the module rules become the Transportation provider. The rules inside the describer today, each a provider hook (or data) with its literal values: the journal entry / line types (`ACC.JournalEntry`, `ACC.EntryLine`: lines shown inside the entry, `PartyType` / `PartyModule` hidden, the line text with account and party), rows named by the user they concern (`CORE.UserCompanyRole`, `CFG.DashboardPin`, `CORE.UserRecoveryCode` → `CORE.User`; `CORE.RolePermission` → `CORE.Role`), the documents named by actions (`DocumentOf`, `DocumentActions`, `ToldByAction`), `Approve.Support` for `CORE.SupportAccessGrant`, the draft-invoice wording (`TRN.Invoice`), the source title codes (`SupplierBill`, `Receivable`), the action extras (journal source, `Accepted`, `LinkApArParty`, `SetupDone` / `SetupReopened`), the "Statement " reason prefix, driver personal fields masked without `drv.view`, code texts (posting-key names from `PostingKeys`, `CORE.Setup.` steps, `TRN.Trip` status Active), company-setting values (`CFG.CompanySetting`, `TRN.ShortageTolerancePct`), the special record names (`NameFromValues`, `TRN.Invoice`, `CORE.Permission`), `ACC.ApArDocument` link kinds, the users' filter lift and last-known-name skip for `CORE.User`, and the personal-field lists of `ErasedRecords`; standard field labels through `FieldCatalogs` (Shared.Infrastructure). The coverage tests in `AuditDescriberTests` stay as they are and green.
  2. Report builder: `ReportBuilderService`, `SavedReportService` with `IReportSourceProvider` (`ReportSources` as the Transportation provider: source keys and field keys unchanged, E-16; the sources are expression trees over Transportation entities and the paid / ownership-type rules of `TripService` / `OwnershipTypeNames`, so they stay in the module); paging DTOs (`PagedResult`, `PageRequest`, `Ref`) and report DTOs → Shared.Contracts; `StandardReportService.ToTable` stays with its module.
  3. Exports: `ExportService` (`Root` = `exports/`, folder format unchanged, E-33) + `ChannelExportQueue` → Shared; its report jobs through the interfaces of step 2.
- **Session 10 — Shared engines, part 5.** **Done 29 Oct 2026** (section above; the paging records committed on their own first). Planned, the rest of session 9, each a green commit with the full proof at the end:
  1. Saved reports: `SavedReportService` (with the dashboard pins) → Shared.Infrastructure behind an interface; its dependencies first — `MasterDataSupport.Decode` / `Token` through `IUrlTokenService`, `AuditTrail.Record` and `ErrorCatalog.Format` through Shared interfaces (`IErrorText`), `ReportName` / `ReportThresholds` / `ReportThresholdsBelow` → `SharedErrorCodes`; the saved-report and pin DTOs (`SavedReportRequest`, `SavedReportResponse`, `SavedReportListQuery`, `DashboardPinRequest`, `DashboardPinResponse`, `PinOrderRequest`, `PinStates`) and the paging DTOs (`PagedResult`, `PageRequest`, `Ref`, used by most services, endpoints, screens and tests: a `using` line each) → Shared.Contracts with the same names (OpenAPI byte-identical). `DashboardService` and the dashboard DTOs stay in Transportation.
  2. Exports: `ExportService` (`Root` = `exports/`, folder format unchanged, E-33) + `ChannelExportQueue` → Shared; its report jobs through `IReportBuilder` and the saved-report interface, the module jobs (standard reports, audit log, lists) through a provider.
- **Session 11 — Platform domain and infrastructure.** **Done 29 Oct 2026** (section above; `UserNames`, `ErrorText` and `ICompanyInfo` → session 12, DT-341). Planned: core.* entities (except `AuditLog` → Shared, `MaintenanceLog` stays for Accounting) and configurations, the permission / plan seed split (same `HasData` values, Ids, PublicIds), identity stores and token providers, e-mail outbox / transports / logo resource, billing fake gateway → Platform.Domain / Platform.Infrastructure; the E-1 anchor exception (`Tenant`, `Company`, `User`); `UserNames` (DT-312), `ErrorText` and the `ICompanyInfo` implementation → Platform.
- **Session 12 — Platform application and contracts (next).** First `UserNames` (DT-312), `ErrorText` and the `ICompanyInfo` implementation → Platform (from session 11); then identity, platform and notification services, `EmailTemplates`, `EmailLayout`, `SystemEmail` (options, interfaces), `IPaymentGateway` / `IPlatformLock` / `IPlatformNumbers`, `TotpCode`, `SignInThrottle`, `TenantPasswordValidator` and `StaffService` (its invitation-token constants are golden, E-26) → Platform.Application, so the temporary reference Platform.Infrastructure → Transportation.Application (DT-339) goes (its layer test then has no exception) and the E-1 test also checks Transportation.Application; DTOs → Platform.Contracts; contribution interfaces `ICompanyProvisioner`, `IDemoDataContributor`, `ISetupStepProvider`, `IUsageMeter`, `ITenantDataEraser`, `IPermissionCatalog`, `INotificationTypeProvider`, with the transport and accounting implementations still in the old projects. Endpoints stay in the host.
- **Session 13 — Shared.UI + Platform.UI.** Design system, shell, client services and `Loc` → Shared.UI (static files still served at today's root URLs); console, tenant, company settings / users / audit log, home, profile and setup-checklist pages → Platform.UI; router `AdditionalAssemblies`; the menu stays static. One focused visual check of the console and tenant screens (AR/EN).
- **Session 14 — Stage 2 close.** Full layer-rule tests (a.2), CI and docs paths, full proof (all tests, OpenAPI re-export, idempotent script, sign-in with the existing key ring, an old link), `BUILD_STATUS.md`, local tag `stage2-done-YYYY-MM-DD` for the owner to push.

If a session runs over its time it ends at a green commit and the rest moves to the next session.

## Launch checklist (open before going live; 26 Oct 2026)
| # | Item | Who |
|---|---|---|
| 1 | Hosting provider and data residency (DEF-01): TLS certificate and domain, encryption at rest (TDE), automatic backups and a restore drill (NFR-05/06, SEC-08, TST-07), availability target (NFR-03), SignalR scale-out if more than one instance | Owner chooses the provider; development sets it up |

3. Module pattern (follow B1):
   - Services in `src/…Application/<Module>/` using `IAppDbContext`, `IRequestContext`, `IChangeContext.SetAction(entity, action, reason)` for audited business actions, `db.InTransactionAsync` for multi-save operations, `db.ExpectRowVersion` for updates, `Fail.*` / `Validator` for problems (codes + AR/EN messages in `Contracts/Errors/ErrorCatalog.cs` — a unit test requires every code to have both messages).
   - DTOs in `src/…Contracts/<Module>/` — identifiers are always URL tokens (`IUrlTokenService.Encode<T>`), never internal ids.
   - Endpoints in `src/…Web/Endpoints/` under the `/companies/{company}` group with `.RequirePermission(PermissionKeys.X)` (or member metadata); add every route to `Web/Pipeline/ApiSummaries.cs` (test-enforced) and register the services in `Application/DependencyInjection.cs`.
   - Use `GrantGuard`, `IPeriodGuard`, `INumberingService` (inside a transaction), `IRecordLockGuard` where the PRD requires.
   - Financial documents (B5 trips, B6 invoices/credit notes, B8 cash documents, B9 AP/AR) post through `PostingEngine.PostOrPendAsync` inside their transaction (`PostingLine.DebitKey/CreditKey` with `PostingKeys.*`, cash/bank by `AccountId`), reverse with `ReverseAsync`, and register an `IPostingSourceHandler` (rebuild entry for retries, attach `JournalEntryId`, describe number + token for links). Parties: `PostingParty("TRN","Customer"|"Driver"|"Partner", id)`. Invoicing must call `PostingEngine.EnsurePostingReadyAsync` (FR-094).
   - Imports: `ISpreadsheetService` + `ImportProblems` / `ImportParse` (`Application/Accounting/ImportSupport.cs`), strict preview/commit, `cfg.ImportBatches` row.
   - Master-data style (B3): `MasterDataSupport` (find by token, `ChooseAsync` for active links, `SaveCreateAsync` for idempotent creates with `clientId`, list status/sort/paging, reference counts + `EnsureUnreferenced`, history from the audit log, market phone/IBAN checks, company-local `TodayAsync`); `StandardFieldService.RulesAsync(entity)` → `FieldRules` (labels, required, hidden → `Keep` stored value); `CustomFieldService.ValidateValuesAsync` for CustomValues (trips will use FieldCatalog.Trip). Avoid public instance methods named `BindAsync`/`TryParse` on services: minimal APIs treat them as parameter binders and every endpoint fails at start-up.
   - Tests: unit (Application.Tests), DB rules (Infrastructure.Tests), API flows + isolation (Api.Tests — the generic isolation and coverage tests pick up new endpoints automatically).
   - After the block: code review + security review (money modules), all tests green, export + lint OpenAPI, `python tools/trace/set_status.py partial|done <IDs>`, a `docs/step4/B<n>_*.md` report, update this file, commit (never push).
4. Pitfalls already met: never edit files containing Arabic with PowerShell `Get-Content/Set-Content` (use the Edit/Write tools); `sqlcmd` needs `-I`; do not re-run `tools/design/gen_efcore.py` (it would overwrite hand-maintained entities/configurations); `UserManager.Set*` persists the user (do not call on unsaved users); creating an authenticator key changes the security stamp.
5. Stops still required: real blockers only. (Step 5 design direction chosen 10 Oct 2026, step 6 mockups approved 10 Oct 2026, D-10 chart template approved and seeded in B2.)
6. Shell pitfall: multi-line `python - <<'EOF'` heredocs containing quotes sometimes fail in the Bash tool ("unexpected EOF"); write the script to the scratchpad with the Write tool and run it. `sed -i` in Git Bash turns CRLF files into LF — restore CRLF afterwards (source files are CRLF).
7. OpenAPI export: `dotnet run --project src/DirectService.Transportation.Web --launch-profile DirectService.Transportation.Web` (the only profile; any other name silently runs as Production without a connection string), then `curl -sk https://localhost:52233/openapi/v1.json -o docs/api/openapi-v1.json` (HTTP redirects to HTTPS); stop the host afterwards.

## How to run (development)
- API host: `dotnet run --project src/DirectService.Transportation.Web` (Development; needs user-secret `UrlTokens:Key` — set on this machine). Health: `/health/ready`; OpenAPI: `/openapi/v1.json`; API reference: `/scalar` (Development only).
- Tests: `dotnet test` (needs local SQL Server; creates `DirectServiceMaster_Tests` and `DirectServiceMaster_ApiTests`).
- OpenAPI lint: export `/openapi/v1.json` to `docs/api/openapi-v1.json`, then `npx @redocly/cli lint docs/api/openapi-v1.json`.

## Environment
- Owner's computer (Windows, `D:\Direct Service Transportation System`). .NET SDK 10.0.401 (`global.json` pins 10.0.401, roll forward latestFeature; tests use Microsoft.Testing.Platform); `dotnet-ef` 10.0.12 global tool; SQL Server 2025 Developer on `localhost` (Windows auth); Node.js 24.20.0 LTS + npm 11.19.0 (installed 8 Oct 2026 via winget); git 2.56 — local repository `main`, author "Direct Service Company <info@directservicetech.com>" (repository-local config), never pushed.
- Databases: `DirectServiceMaster` (development; collation `Arabic_100_CI_AI_SC_UTF8`, READ_COMMITTED_SNAPSHOT ON); `DirectServiceMaster_Tests` (recreated by the integration tests on every run).
- `sqlcmd` needs `-I` (QUOTED_IDENTIFIER ON) for any DML on tables with filtered indexes.
- Packages managed centrally in `Directory.Packages.props`; warnings are errors (`Directory.Build.props`); NuGet audit on every restore (SEC-15).
