## Decisions taken without asking (DT-01…DT-335 all accepted by the owner; newer rows for owner review)
| # | Topic | Options considered | Chosen |
|---|---|---|---|
| DT-01 | Names of the 300 standard foreign keys (design lists them without names) | (a) design convention `FK_<Table>_<RefTable>_<Column>`; (b) EF default names | (a), e.g. `FK_TRN_Trips_Users_CreatedBy` |
| DT-02 | Names of multi-column check constraints | (a) `CK_<Table>_<Col1>_<Col2>…` per convention; (b) short descriptive names | (a), e.g. `CK_TRN_Trips_VoidReason_VoidedBy_VoidedAt`, `CK_<Table>_DeletedAt_DeletedBy_DeleteReason` |
| DT-03 | ISJSON check on JSON columns whose rule cell does not say CHECK (`TRN_InvoiceLayouts.Labels/Signatures`, `CustomFieldDefinitions.Options`) | (a) add (design §3.3 + build prompt: every JSON column has an ISJSON check); (b) omit | (a). E-invoice `RequestPayload/ResponsePayload` ("JSON/XML text") have no ISJSON check because UBL is XML |
| DT-04 | "Required when …" rules without "(CHECK)" (e.g. `TRN_Invoices.ApprovedBy`, `SubscriptionInvoices.PaidAt`, `ApArEInvoiceSubmissions` document pairing, `Accounts.ControlPartyType` required for control accounts) | (a) application rules only, as the design marks them; (b) add database checks | (a) — enforced by the services; candidates for extra checks later if wanted |
| DT-05 | EF Core's automatic index per foreign key (~300 unlisted indexes) | (a) disable — only the design's indexes; (b) keep and list as extras | (a) |
| DT-06 | EF migrations history table | (a) `dbo.__EFMigrationsHistory` (EF infrastructure, outside the design schemas); (b) inside `core` | (a) |
| DT-07 | `TRN_Tariffs.Specificity` persisted computed column (A-07) | expression wrapped in `ISNULL(…, 0)` so SQL Server infers NOT NULL as designed | as stated |
| DT-08 | TRIAL plan limits (placeholders, D-12) | — | Price 0, Monthly, MaxCompanies 2, MaxActiveUsers 5, MaxTripsPerMonth 2,000, MaxStorageMb 1,024, IsActive 0 (not for sale). Owner edits in the provider console |
| DT-09 | Permission `GroupName` and Arabic descriptions | — | English group names from PRD 3.4 groups; Arabic descriptions translated from the PRD "Grants" text (`ReferenceDataSeed.cs`) |
| DT-10 | Database defaults vs EF insert behaviour | (a) defaults declared in the database, but the application always sends the value (`ValueGeneratedNever`), entity initialisers mirror the design defaults; (b) rely on database defaults (EF "sentinel" problem: a `bool` defaulting to 1 could never be inserted as 0) | (a) |
| DT-11 | READ_COMMITTED_SNAPSHOT | (a) ON (readers never block writers; 25 concurrent users); (b) server default | (a) — in `00_CreateDatabase.sql`, local and test databases. Numbering still uses `UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK` |
| DT-12 | Audit rows of inserted records need their identity values | (a) audit rows saved right after the main save inside the same transaction (the interceptor starts one if the caller has not); (b) a separate transaction | (a) — the document and its audit rows commit or roll back together |
| DT-13 | Rows not written to `core.AuditLogs` | `core.UserSessions` (LastSeenAt every minute) and `cfg.RecordLocks` (heartbeats) — technical, high-frequency, not business events; sign-ins/sign-outs are audited explicitly by the identity services | as stated |
| DT-15 | Names of preset roles and of the built-in ownership type (stored values) | (a) fixed English codes (`Owner`, `Administrator`, `Accountant`, `Dispatcher`, `Viewer`; ownership type `Company`) translated by the UI; (b) Arabic text in the database | (a) — presets cannot be renamed (FR-173, C-08); the UI shows them in the user's language |
| DT-16 | Notification text for "invitation accepted" (FR-190) | Bilingual message stored in `core.Notifications.Message` ("… قبل الدعوة / accepted the invitation") until the notifications module (B11) renders per language | as stated |
| DT-17 | Tenant-level permission (core.tenant.manage) on `/api/v1/tenant/*` | (a) held in at least one active company of the tenant; (b) a separate tenant role | (a) — no schema change; Owner preset holds it |
| DT-18 | Privilege boundaries in user administration (security review SR-01) | (a) a caller can grant only keys it holds; only tenant.manage holders act on Owner-level users; (b) PRD wording only ("users.manage can … assign roles") | (a) — prevents an Administrator from making itself Owner; the PRD does not allow escalation explicitly or implicitly |
| DT-19 | Archived company visibility | Visible (read-only) only to users holding core.tenant.manage; others get 404 | as stated (D-09: "only the Owner sees it") |
| DT-20 | Authenticator code replay within its 30 s window (SR-04) | (a) accept ASP.NET Identity default for now; (b) store the last used time-step (needs a column — schema change) | (a) — listed as a Suggestion in the B1 review |
| DT-21 | FR-094 "posting disabled" scope | (a) automatic posting and invoicing only; (b) also manual and opening entries | (a) — manual and opening entries name their accounts; the opening entry needs the difference key only when unbalanced |
| DT-22 | Date of reversal entries (voids, edits) | (a) the original entry's date; (b) the void date | (a) — the void/edit nets to zero in the original's period; voiding is refused when that period is closed (FR-133) |
| DT-23 | "Until … the first period is closed" (FR-110) and reopen stamps | (a) setup ends once any period has ever been closed; ClosedBy/ClosedAt record the latest close or reopen; (b) only while a period is currently closed | (a) |
| DT-24 | Opening balances on control accounts (no party column for openings) | (a) opening entry lines carry no party; the pre-close check lists them until allocated to parties by manual entries; (b) refuse openings on control accounts | (a) — FR-106 targets manual journal lines |
| DT-25 | Cash box / bank ledger account | Each needs its own active, postable, non-control Asset leaf account, not shared with another cash box or bank account ("one account per cash box / bank account", 7.5.1) | as stated |
| DT-26 | Chart import and existing codes ("preview rejects duplicate codes") | (a) duplicates within the file and codes already in the chart are rejected; import adds new accounts only; (b) update existing accounts | (a) — openings of existing accounts are edited on the opening-balances screen |
| DT-27 | Party identification in the journal import | Natural keys: customer name, driver national ID, partner name, accounting-party name (type "Supplier" = role Supplier); ambiguous keys rejected | as stated |
| DT-28 | Edit history link for manual entries (D-05) — no column links the corrected entry to the original | Audit rows: original "Reverse" (reason = reversal number), corrected entry "Edit" (reason "JV-x → JV-y"); the reversal links both ways in the table | as stated |
| DT-29 | Pre-close check contents (FR-134) | Pending postings = all open ones (they carry no date); unapproved drafts = TRN invoices in Draft dated in the month; unreconciled = control-account amounts without party up to month end (full party reconciliation RPT-23 in B9) | as stated |
| DT-30 | Period rules | Months without a row are Open; closing a future month is refused; close/reopen need settings.manage + reason | as stated |
| DT-31 | Journal export content | All entries of the period range (posted, reversed, void, reversals); entries point to the latest batch; re-export makes a new batch; the file is returned when created | as stated |
| DT-32 | Permissions of B2 screens | Mapping and periods: core.settings.manage; journal list: acc.acct.view; automatic-entries list: acc.je.view (automatic entries only); retry and export: acc.je.export; account-status banner: every company member | as stated (PRD 3.4) |
| DT-33 | Pending-posting worker | Every 5 min, back-off 2^attempts min (max 60), oldest first, skips suspended tenants and archived companies; acts as a system job | as stated |
| DT-34 | Account changes once entries exist | Changing type, control flag or control party type is refused once the account has entry lines; deactivation refused with openings, links or active children | as stated |
| DT-35 | Reversal entries of manual entries | Cannot be edited or voided themselves (CR-B2-01) | as stated |
| DT-36 | Read access to master data (PRD 3.4 has no masters.view) | (a) driver and partner records need drv.view or masters.manage; customer, material, site and ownership-type lists, every pick list ("options") and the form configuration are open to company members; writes masters.manage; field configuration and company default column views settings.manage; personal column views any member; (b) masters.manage for all reads | (a) — every role needs names to read trips, invoices and reports; personal data (national ID, IBAN) stays behind drv.view |
| DT-37 | Normalisation of keys | Names, national IDs, phones, IBANs, plates stored with ASCII digits; plates upper case without spaces; phones/IBANs without spaces or dashes; uniqueness checked on the stored values (collation CI_AI) | as stated |
| DT-38 | Ownership types have no Status column | Not archived; deleted (soft) only while unreferenced; built-in Company cannot be renamed, reclassified or deleted (design + FR-161) | as stated |
| DT-39 | Profile account figures (FR-156…158) | Filled when the documents exist (B6 invoices/credit notes, B8 receipts/advances/settlements/partner payments); B3 profile = record + reference counts + history | as stated |
| DT-40 | Restoring an archived customer/partner whose name an active record now uses | Refused (duplicate.key) until one is renamed | as stated |
| DT-41 | Custom values storage | Only non-empty values stored; hidden fields keep stored values (client value ignored); unknown keys refused; default applied on create when absent; "has data" = a non-deleted record stores the key; FieldKey fixed after creation | as stated |
| DT-42 | Deleting a custom field | Without data → soft delete with reason; with data → refused, hide offered (flow 9.14); a used List option cannot be removed | as stated |
| DT-43 | Standard-field rules | Core trip fields (US-27) and business-required fields cannot be hidden or made optional; conditional fields (driver bank name, IBAN, CliQ alias) cannot be hidden; partner cannot be made always required; optional fields can be renamed, hidden, required, reordered; hidden optional fields keep their stored values on edit; one replacement label (design has one Label column) shown in both languages | as stated |
| DT-44 | Master-data imports | Strict (any bad row blocks) and add-only (existing keys = duplicates), like DT-26; column matching by header aliases (Arabic, English, field key, company label) or an explicit `mapping` (field key → header); links (ownership type, partner) by name, active only | as stated |
| DT-45 | PDF export of lists (FR-165) | With the print engine in B6; Excel/CSV now | as stated |
| DT-46 | Driver payment details when Cash | Bank/IBAN/CliQ kept as entered and format-checked when given; required only for their method | as stated |
| DT-47 | Expiry alert window | Expired = before today; expiring = today … today + 30 days inclusive; "today" in the company time zone | as stated |
| DT-48 | Partner on an own-fleet driver | Refused (a partner's truck is external fleet, BR-029); an ownership type with partner-linked drivers cannot become own fleet | as stated |
| DT-49 | Idempotent create (API-05) | Implemented from B3 on: `clientId` GUID → PublicId; repeat → 200 with the record, new → 201; a client id that exists outside the company → 400 | as stated |
| DT-50 | Read access to pricing (PRD 3.4 has no pricing view key) | (a) tariffs, monthly rates, their history and exports readable with masters.manage, trips.view, trips.edit or reports.view; quote with trips.edit or masters.manage; missing-rates warning any member; writes/imports masters.manage; (b) masters.manage only | (a) — trip viewers see prices and wages on trips anyway; the breach report (reports.view) needs tariffs |
| DT-51 | Tariff delete | (a) archive/restore only (FR-019 lists archive; nothing references tariffs); (b) soft delete too | (a) — archived lines stay in the design's unique index, so an identical line is edited or restored instead |
| DT-52 | Tariff edit semantics | (a) edit in place, old → new in TRN_RateHistory and core.AuditLogs; party type, keys and dates editable; (b) new version row per change | (a) — trips store their prices, so edits never alter saved trips (FR-026) |
| DT-53 | Month-strip status | A site counts as rated with at least one of the two rates (design allows one); Complete = every active loading site rated; Partial = some; None = none (or no active site) | as stated |
| DT-54 | Driver rate of partner trips | 0 regardless of the monthly table (7.1.1, FR-005); stored RateSource 'Monthly' (CHECK allows Monthly/Manual; nothing was typed) | as stated (applies in B5) |
| DT-55 | Typed driver rate without masters.manage | No monthly rate → blocked with the missing-rate message (FR-030 "otherwise"); differs from the monthly rate → refused `rate.manualNotAllowed` (BR-004) | as stated (applies in B5) |
| DT-56 | Dates used by pricing | Tariff validity checked on the trip date (FR-020); rate month = loading date's month (BR-029); the quote uses one for the other when only one is given | as stated |
| DT-57 | Editing rates of past or closed months | Allowed (rates are configuration and never alter saved trips; trips cannot be posted into closed periods anyway) | as stated |
| DT-58 | FR-034 "warns" | Non-blocking: GET shows `hasTrips` and trip counts per site so the UI warns before saving; the save response repeats the warning. "Trips" = non-void trips with that rate month | as stated |
| DT-59 | FR-035 "clearing both rates" | Both empty → soft delete with reason "Rates cleared", refused while non-void trips of that site use the month; removing one of two rates is allowed | as stated |
| DT-60 | Rate history format | Readable snapshots (names at the time, amounts invariant, All = empty) + action (Create/Update/Archive/Restore/Clear) and subject; RateHistory rows are not copied into core.AuditLogs (the tariff/rate rows are audited there) | as stated |
| DT-61 | Decimals of prices and rates | Company currency decimals (3 for JOD; PRD 7.2.1 "Money, 3 decimals"), although the columns hold 4 | as stated |
| DT-62 | Archived loading sites in the month grid | Keep and show their rates in months where they have them; get no new rates; the grid lists active sites | as stated |
| DT-63 | "Save and print" of monthly rates (FR-028) | Excel/CSV now; PDF with the print engine in B6 (like DT-45) | as stated |
| DT-64 | Concurrency on the monthly grid (B4 review M1) | Changing or clearing stored rates requires the row version (400 missing, 409 stale) | as stated — stricter than other PUTs (B3 L4 still open for those) |
| DT-65 | Digits and dates in the Arabic UI | Western digits isolated as LTR runs; default date format YYYY-MM-DD (user preference can change it, FR-178) | as stated |
| DT-66 | Menu placement of setup checklist, tenant group, provider console | Setup checklist top-level while setup is incomplete; Tenant group only for core.tenant.manage; provider console in its own shell | as stated |
| DT-67 | Default table density | Compact (32 px rows / 32 px controls); comfortable 40 / 36 px | as stated |
| DT-68 | Storage of saved views (owner chose C's saved views; no table in the design) | (a) rows of cfg.TableColumnViews under namespaced keys `<list>#view:<name>`, Columns JSON = columns + filters + name; personal (UserId) or company-shared (UserId NULL, settings.manage); built-in views defined in code; (b) new table (schema change) | (a) — no schema change |
| DT-69 | Storage of the density preference (no column in core.Users) | (a) per device in browser storage; (b) new column (schema change) | (a) — shown in the profile as "saved on this device" |
| DT-65 (amended) | Date format default | The database design defaults `core.Users.DateFormat` to `dd/MM/yyyy`; that default stays (design contract). The board's YYYY-MM-DD dates are illustrative; each user chooses the format (FR-178) | as stated |
| DT-70 | Quick-entry memory (FR-007) — no column in the design | Driver, customer, material and route of the user's last saved trip (works across sessions and devices, no schema change) | as stated |
| DT-71 | Partner on imported rows without a partner cell | The driver's partner, as the form pre-fills it (7.1.1); a tolerant import with an unknown partner also falls back to it and flags the row | as stated |
| DT-72 | Strict vs tolerant trip import (FR-013) | Strict: any rejected row blocks the import. Tolerant: invalid optional values (unloaded weight, unloading/loading date, partner, custom values) take defaults and flag Needs review with the fields named; rows with errors in required values are skipped and reported | as stated |
| DT-73 | Row version on trip changes | Required on edit, void and clear-review (400 missing, 409 stale), so invoicing/settling meanwhile refuses stale changes | as stated |
| DT-74 | Re-resolution on edit (FR-032, FR-026) | When site/month/driver/fleet (rate) or customer/material/sites/date (tariff) change, a rate or price resent unchanged by the form counts as not typed and the new value applies; a different typed value follows the manual/lock rules | as stated |
| DT-75 | Trip list search (FR-009 "search") with 500,000 trips (NFR-01) | Any part of the receipt number; drivers (with plates), customers, partners, sites and materials by their filters; plates and names also through the Ctrl+K global search (FR-187, B11) | as stated |
| DT-76 | Loading date in a closed period | Refused, as PRD 8.9 requires for trip and loading dates (7.1.1 lists it only for the trip date) | as stated |
| DT-77 | Amounts visible to trips.view | Trip amounts (wage, partner cost, margin) are part of the trip a Viewer may view (PRD 3.4) | as stated |
| DT-78 | Edit re-posting | The wage/partner-cost entry is reversed and re-posted only when what it posts changes (amount, party, date, receipt) | as stated |
| DT-79 | Tax-inclusive invoices (company setting `TaxInclusive`, BR-022) | Lines, discount and fees are entered tax-included; each is converted to net separately (half-up), base = net lines − net discount + net fees, tax = gross − base, total = gross. The stored amounts satisfy the design's CHECKs and revenue/other revenue post net. Credit notes follow the invoice's own mode | as stated |
| DT-80 | Credit note over the remaining balance by rounding (with receipts) | A proportional credit note above the remaining balance by up to 0.010 takes exactly the remaining balance (difference in its tax); beyond that BR-026 refuses it | as stated |
| DT-81 | "Correct and resubmit" (D-04) for an approved document | Amounts of an approved invoice cannot change: correction means fixing customer, company or e-invoicing settings data; the document is rebuilt from current data with the same UUID and ICV. A wrong amount is handled by void (never accepted) or credit note + new invoice | as stated |
| DT-82 | National lines vs one discount/tax per document | Each line is sent as quantity 1 × its net amount (quantity × price in the description); stored net subtotal, discount and fees are split over the lines exactly; fees are a separate line; tax per line, with the rounding difference to the stored tax spread one smallest unit per line — the national document always equals what was posted and printed | as stated; tolerance to confirm on the JoFotara test environment |
| DT-83 | When the ICV is taken | At queueing, inside the approval / credit-note transaction (gap-free with the document). Cancelled documents leave values never sent | to confirm with ISTD (R-01) |
| DT-84 | Payment-method digit of the JoFotara type | Receivable when the due date is after the invoice date, otherwise cash | as stated |
| DT-85 | Retry policy | Problems found before sending: stays Queued, no attempt counted, re-checked every 10 minutes and on manual submission. Technical failures: back-off 2, 4, 8 … ≤ 60 min; after 5 attempts Rejected with the last error ("Failed"). Refusals: Rejected at once | as stated |
| DT-86 | Buyer identification and address | Tax number sent as TN when known, otherwise omitted (no national-ID/passport fields in the design); governorate code from the customer's city (12 governorates, Arabic/English), otherwise omitted; phone sent when 9–14 digits | as stated |
| DT-87 | Simulator connector | Development and tests only: offered when `EInvoicing:AllowSimulator` is set, never with the Production environment | as stated |
| DT-88 | Who sees e-invoicing | Report/list: inv.view or reports.view (PRD 3.4 "all reports"); a document's submission: inv.view; submit/resubmit: inv.issue; settings: settings.manage | as stated |
| DT-89 | Void of an accepted credit note | Refused (by analogy with FR-046); a pending one is cancelled | as stated |
| DT-90 | Void while the outcome is unknown | Refused (`einvoice.outcomeUnknown`) while an attempt is in flight or ended in a technical failure, until a resubmission answers (same UUID → ALREADY_SUBMITTED if received) | as stated |
| DT-91 | Changing connector or environment | Refused while documents are queued or rejected; documents accepted in Test keep their status and the counter continues — use a test company for test-environment trials | as stated |
| DT-14 (B7) | Audit of submissions | `RequestPayload`, `ResponsePayload`, `QrCode` written as `"***"` (kept on the submission row itself) | as stated |
| DT-92 | Receipt rules | Against an invoice: the customer's Approved invoice with remaining > 0 (a Closed invoice takes no receipt); on account: active customer. Totals of the lists count active documents only | as stated |
| DT-93 | Settlement scope | Only own-fleet trips (partner trips are paid through the partner account); at least one trip or advance; deduction lines of 0 are not stored (design CHECK > 0) | as stated |
| DT-94 | Accrued vs not accrued at settlement (BR-031a) | Per trip: wage entry posted (not reversed) or waiting as a pending posting → Dr driver payable; otherwise Dr driver wage expense. A settled trip's pending posting always accrues | as stated |
| DT-95 | Concurrency of cash documents | Each document takes its own sequence number first; checks that depend on other documents (invoice remaining balance, partner balance due, open settlement items) run after it, under that lock, and row versions turn any remaining race into 409 | as stated |
| DT-96 | Shortage suggestion (FR-078, no formula in the PRD) | (shortage − loaded × tolerance %) × the trip's price per ton, half-up; editable or removable by the user | as stated |
| DT-97 | Archived parties on cash documents | Archived drivers can be settled, archived partners paid, archived customers can pay open invoices; new advances and on-account receipts need active parties | as stated |
| DT-98 | "Copy" on reprint (FR-090) | Any earlier print of the document in the audit log marks the print "Copy"; statements are not marked | as stated |
| DT-99 | Driver / partner balance | What the company owes: driver = wages of active own-fleet trips − active advances − settlement deductions − settlement nets; partner = partner cost of active trips − active payments | as stated |
| DT-100 | Partner trip changes after payment | Voiding a partner trip or lowering its cost is refused when the partner would be overpaid (checked under the partner-payment lock) | as stated |
| DT-101 | Transport customers as receivable parties (FR-122) | (a) the first receivable of a transport customer creates an accounting Customer party from its data (name, tax number, phone, address, terms; control account = trade receivables) and links it; (b) require the link to be made first | (a) — audited ("LinkApArParty"); party options list accounting customers and transport customers |
| DT-102 | Tax number on parties receiving a tax invoice (design rule) | ~~A receivable with tax > 0 is refused while e-invoicing is on and the party has no tax number~~ | **Changed by the owner (11 Oct 2026): same rule as DT-86** — the buyer's tax number is sent (TN) when known and may be missing (PRD 7.8.2); the refusal and error `apar.taxNumberRequired` were removed. "Buyer without tax number accepted?" is a JoFotara test item (open issues) |
| DT-103 | "Current period" result in the balance sheet (no fiscal year in the design) | Calendar year: 1 January … as-of date; earlier unclosed results shown as a separate equity line | as stated |
| DT-104 | Sub-ledger reconciliation (FR-105) | Per control account (all of them): ledger vs party balances on its lines (lines without / with a wrong party listed) and vs the sub-ledger rebuilt from documents as of the date (AP/AR documents, transport invoices less on-account receipts, partner payable, unsettled advances); driver payable line-based only; open pending postings counted | as stated (review M2) |
| DT-105 | Maintenance actions (FR-135/136) | Unlinked = no source document and not the opening entry; reversal partners together; delete-all keeps linked and opening entries; replace-chart only when nothing is posted, no pending postings, AP/AR documents or parties (chart, mapping, cash boxes, banks soft-deleted; template or empty); full reset = entries, openings, periods, back to initial setup — refused with linked entries, closed periods or pending postings; word «حذف» / DELETE; set-based soft deletes logged in core.MaintenanceLogs plus one audit row per action (not per record); JSON backup (POST, logged) | as stated |
| DT-106 | AP/AR drafts and edits (mockup shows "save as draft") | No drafts (design status Posted/Void) and no edit: void + new document, or a return | as stated; **owner (11 Oct 2026): "Save as draft" removed from the AP/AR screens** (board SCR-40/41 updated) |
| DT-107 | AP/AR lines and tax | Bills: expense or asset accounts; receivables: revenue; never control accounts; line rate defaults to the company rate; document tax = Σ amount × rate rounded once; returns take the proportional tax share, a full return the tax not yet returned; return and payment dates not before the document date | as stated |
| DT-108 | Permissions of B9 | AP/AR reads acct.apar or acct.view, writes and e-invoice submit acct.apar; ageing also reports.view; GL, trial balance, P&L, balance sheet, cost centres acct.view; account statement, cash/bank statement and balances, reconciliation acct.view or reports.view (7.7.3); maintenance acct.maint | as stated |
| DT-109 | Ageing as of a date | Documents dated up to the date less payments/returns (receipts/credit notes) dated up to it; transport invoices Approved/Closed; on-account receipts as unapplied | as stated |
| DT-110 | Accounting party names | Unique among active parties of the same role; role and control account fixed once documents or journal lines use the party | as stated (review M1) |
| DT-111 | P&L comparison period | Optional second period (approved mockup SCR-37) | as stated |
| DT-112 | AP/AR numbering | One PAY- sequence for payments and collections, one RTN- for both return kinds (the design has one unique number per company) | as stated |
| DT-113 | Account statement vs general ledger | Same engine; the statement adds the party filter and is also open to reports.view | as stated |
| DT-114 | Tonnage in operational reports and the dashboard | Loaded weight of active trips by trip date | as stated (B10) |
| DT-115 | Default status in the report builder | Sources with a status show active (approved/closed invoices, posted credit notes, active documents and records) unless the definition filters on status | as stated |
| DT-116 | Shared saved reports (FR-145 "owner or administrator") | Administrator = holder of settings.manage; private reports only for their owner | as stated |
| DT-117 | Report permissions | reports.view ("all reports", PRD 3.4) opens every standard report and the cash/bank cards; without it a report needs its module's view key; builder sources always need their own view key (FR-147); dashboard parts each need their key | as stated (review B10-6) |
| DT-118 | Global search by permission | Trips trips.view; invoices/credit notes inv.view; receipts rcpt.manage/inv.view; advances, settlements, partner payments drv.*; AP/AR documents and parties acct.apar/acct.view; journal entries acct.view/je.view; drivers and partners drv.view/masters.manage (national ID only with drv.view); customers every member | as stated (B11) |
| DT-119 | Tariff breach "by partner" (FR-024) | Trips store the customer tariff only (FR-022/023): the breach is customer price vs tariff; the partner option lists the breaches of that partner's trips | as stated (review B10-1) |
| DT-120 | Notification text | Both languages stored as readable JSON {"Ar","En"} within the 500-character column, shown in the reader's language; earlier plain-text messages shown as stored | as stated |
| DT-121 | Who reads the audit log (FR-189) | settings.manage or users.manage; the company's rows plus the tenant's user/role/permission/invitation/sign-in rows; driver personal fields masked without drv.view | as stated |
| DT-122 | Background exports (FR-151) | Requested explicitly (`POST /exports`); kinds: report builder, saved report, standard report, audit log; at most 3 waiting per user; kept 7 days; builder detail up to 100,000 rows in the background (5,000 on screen) | as stated |
| DT-123 | Downgrade timing and scope (5.3 "downgrade at renewal") | Only to a cheaper plan of the same billing cycle with no higher limit; refused while usage exceeds the target (BR-012); the lower limits apply at once, the new price from the next renewal (nothing to refund mid-period) | as stated (B12, review F1) |
| DT-124 | Plan of a checkout (no plan column on subscription invoices) | The plan (by public id) and the invoice kind (period / upgrade) travel as gateway metadata and come back in the signed webhook; a new checkout soft-deletes the tenant's unpaid (Due or Failed) invoices ("Replaced by a new checkout"); an upgrade is applied only while the tenant is Active in the same period; a payment is applied only for the invoice's exact total and currency, otherwise the invoice is Failed | as stated (review F2, F3) |
| DT-125 | Suspension date | Kept in `GraceEndsAt` (trial end or grace end, or the staff suspension time) — the 30-day clock to Cancelled reads it | as stated |
| DT-126 | Support session (D-13) | View permissions only; writes refused (403) and exports refused; staff see only the tenant's active companies; every request audited as "SupportRequest" with the status returned; the Owner can end the window early | as stated (review F6, F14) |
| DT-127 | System account for system jobs (open issue B1) | `system@platform.invalid`: platform staff, Disabled, no password, created on first use; `DeletedBy` of retention deletions and actor of lifecycle changes | as stated |
| DT-128 | Storage limit | Measured (stored export files) and shown with usage; not enforced until the platform stores user files (attachments are not in scope) | as stated |

| DT-187 | Company switch kept the old company's data on screen (owner bug 14 Oct 2026) | Cause (client only): the router re-renders the layout with the new page *before* the layout's location handler selects the new company, so the new page's first request (`Api.C` → `ShellState.Company`) still went to the old company and nothing reloaded it; the server had no cache keyed without the company (the only server caches are grants per user and sessions per token; every request resolves the company from its route). Options: (a) central — the layout starts the switch in `OnParametersSet`, builds no page until the URL's company is selected (a page is then always new for that company), `ShellState.CompanyToken` cancels the old company's reads, `Api` drops any read answer whose company is no longer on screen (`OperationCanceledException`, ignored by Blazor), changes the user started finish where they were sent; shell figures (badge, setup) reset and are guarded, lookups (trip, money accounts, accounting) never keep or overwrite with another company's lists, record locks are released in the company they were taken in, live-update group joins serialised; (b) make every page reload on a company-changed event | (a) — one place for all screens; plus `Cache-Control: no-store` on every `/api` answer (defence in depth: the browser never keeps one company's answer) |
| DT-188 | Readable history and audit log (owner feedback 14 Oct 2026); presentation only, nothing stored changes | (a) one server describer (`AuditDescriber`) used by every History panel, the audit log and its exports: each row as a sentence in the reader's language, actions/record types/fields/codes from `AuditVocabulary` (screen labels; trips/drivers/customers use the company's Field-configuration labels and custom-field labels), ids resolved to numbers and names inside the reader's tenant and company (soft-deleted records by name; records no longer found by the last name the log recorded), amounts 3 decimals, dates DD/MM/YYYY, yes/no, "—"; technical columns hidden; a journal entry and the lines saved with it are one event with a lines table; documents an action names (invoice, settlement, credit note, receipt, payment, return, reversal, source of an entry) are links, shown as links only when the reader's permissions open that screen; (b) translate on the client | (a), with these choices: **Arabic sentences use the verbal noun after the user's name ("علي علي: إضافة الرحلة …")** so no sentence guesses the user's gender, English uses the past tense ("Ali Ali added the trip …"); a trip added to a draft that is numbered since reads "draft invoice INV-00001"; a record's history does not list the values of its creation (the sentence says it), the audit log does; customers and partners are not linked (their panel has no address); **"Technical details" (stored values as they are) only for settings.manage**, on request, on screen and in exports (exports are readable by default); changes read "label: old ← new" in Arabic and "old → new" in English with every value bidi-isolated. **Temporary-id bug**: the audit interceptor captured new values before the save, when keys of rows inserted in the same save (an entry posted with its document, its lines, a new parent) were EF's temporary negative numbers; it now re-reads the captured columns after the save, so the log holds the saved ids. Old rows with a temporary key show "—" for it, and lines written that way still join their entry (same save time). Tariff and monthly-rate histories (own log) get the same sentences, labels and formats |
| DT-190 | Users created by an administrator with a username and password (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, item 1) | (a) username column next to an optional email, sign-in field accepts either; (b) usernames stored in the email column | (a): `Email` becomes nullable (revision 6). Choices made: username 3–50 ASCII letters/digits/`.`/`_`/`-`, case-insensitive, unique platform-wide (never contains "@", so never equal to an email); such users need **no email verification** (the administrator vouches) and **no 2FA** — everyone else keeps the 2FA rule until item 2; "Must change password at first login" ON by default, enforced as a sign-in stage before the second step (policy applies, must differ from the current password); **"Set a new password" by an administrator only for users created with a username** (users who sign in with their email keep the emailed link — an administrator never learns their password); setting it unlocks the account, ends every session and asks for a new password by default; a user without email gets no reset email (silent, no enumeration) and changes the password in the profile with the current one; anyone can turn 2FA on from the profile (QR + first code + 10 recovery codes; this session stays signed in, other sessions end) and cannot turn it off themselves (an administrator can reset it); the plan's user limit and DT-18 (no role beyond the creator's keys) apply; audit actions `CreatedWithPassword`, `PasswordSetByAdmin`, `PasswordChanged`, `TwoFactorEnabled` |
| DT-191 | 2FA optional at sign-up; "Require 2FA for all users" (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, item 2) | (a) setting on the tenant, next to the password policy (one sign-in policy for all companies of the tenant; Owner = core.tenant.manage); (b) per company | (a) — sign-in happens before a company is chosen and a user can belong to several companies; the toggle is on Tenant → Companies → Security. 2FA required = platform staff (always) or the tenant setting; otherwise the set-up is **offered once**, at the first sign-in of a user who signs in with an email (sign-up Owner, invited users) — not to users created with a username (the administrator chose; the profile recommends it); "Skip for now" is audited; turning the setting on ends the sessions of users without 2FA (security review) and they must set it up at the next sign-in; users keep 2FA once on (only an administrator's reset removes it); after an administrator's 2FA reset the user signs in without a code unless the tenant requires it (existing test changed accordingly) |
| DT-192 | Security review of the authentication changes (items 1–2) | — | Done, `docs/reviews/2026-10-17_Auth_Security_Review.md`: 11 areas checked, one fix (sessions end when the requirement is turned on); residual risks for the owner: admin-entered emails are not verified (reset link could go to a mistyped address), 2FA optional by default |
| DT-193 | Journal entry drafts and Reference (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, item 3) | (a) drafts in their own table `acc.JournalDrafts` (lines as JSON with internal ids), posted through the normal engine; (b) a "Draft" status on `acc.JournalEntries` with an empty number | (a) — no report, balance, period check or export can ever count a draft, numbering stays gap-free by construction. Choices made: a draft may be incomplete (missing date, accounts, unbalanced) but needs a description or a line; posting runs every manual-entry rule and the period lock at that moment, and a refused post keeps the draft; drafts are listed first under "All" and alone under "Drafts" (filters a draft cannot match — number, automatic, source type — leave them out); deleting a draft is a soft delete without a reason; Reference is free text up to 100 characters, copied to reversals, searchable in the journal list and the global search, shown in the entry view, voucher, account statement and every journal export; in the journal import the new column is "Reference / المرجع" (one value per entry), so "Reference" is no longer accepted as a name of the grouping column "Entry ref" |
| DT-194 | Receipts against several invoices (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, item 4) | (a) allocation rows `trn.TRN_ReceiptAllocations` as the single source of what a receipt paid, existing receipts migrated; (b) keep `TRN_Receipts.InvoiceId` and add rows only for extra invoices | (a) — every "paid as of", "unapplied" and "has receipts" query reads one place. Choices made: `TRN_Receipts.InvoiceId` stays filled only when exactly one invoice is allocated (links and older clients); the unallocated part of a receipt stays on account (unapplied in the ageing and statements); allocations are in invoice-date order, filled oldest first on screen and editable; an empty amount follows the total of the ticked invoices; a void keeps the allocation rows as the record and gives each invoice its amount back; the journal entry stays one line Dr cash/bank, Cr trade receivables for the whole amount, its text names up to three invoices |
| DT-195 | Trial balance tree (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, item 5) | (a) parent rows = column-by-column sum of the children (debit and credit summed separately), totals = rows of the chosen level; (b) parent rows with the net balance of their subtree | (a) — the owner asked for "parent rows = sum of children" and the roots then add up exactly to the totals, which balance as before. The level filter is the depth of the tree; the tree opens fully expanded; the flat list stays available to other callers (`tree` omitted) |
| DT-196 | Account statement of several accounts replaces the General Ledger (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, item 6) | (a) a new endpoint for several accounts next to the single-account statement; (b) widen the single-account endpoint | (a) — the single-account statement and the general-ledger endpoints keep their contracts for integrations; only the screen changed. Choices made: chosen accounts are shown as chosen (a group covers its sub-accounts) and a group together with one of its own sub-accounts is refused so the grand total counts every line once; a code range and "all accounts" list accounts without sub-accounts (the range end includes codes that start with it) and leave out accounts with no opening and no movement; party filter only for one chosen control account; one request, at most 20,000 lines; the old `/accounting/ledger` address redirects to the statement with the same query (bookmarks, links); no stored report or view referred to the ledger page, so nothing had to be migrated |
| DT-197 | Supplier bill line deductions (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, item 7) | (a) the "Supplier deductions" key optional (needed only by a bill with a deduction); (b) required like the others | (a) — every company created before revision 6 would otherwise stop posting until it mapped the new key. New companies get account 4105 "Supplier deductions income" (Revenue, like driver deductions 4103) mapped by the template; older companies map it in Account mapping before their first deduction. Choices made: deductions only on supplier bills; the document keeps Subtotal (gross) and Tax (on the full amounts) and stores Σ deductions, Total and Remaining are the payable; the supplier's account statement shows each bill net with the deduction in the line text. **For the owner:** (1) returns on a bill with deductions are still capped at the bill's remaining payable and do not give back the deduction — a return of the whole bill can be refused when the deduction is larger than the tax; (2) there is no separate supplier statement with a deductions column (the account statement filtered by the supplier is used) |
| DT-198 | Budgets (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, part B, feature 1) | Fiscal year: (a) calendar year; (b) a new fiscal-year setting. Accounts: (a) revenue and expense only; (b) any type. Several approved: (a) refuse while another is approved (return it to draft first); (b) approving replaces the old one silently. Report: (a) approved budget only; (b) any budget | (a) in each case. Also: amounts in the natural direction of the account, negatives allowed (contra accounts such as 4104 discount allowed); a line on a parent account and one on its sub-account for the same cost centre is refused (no double counting); an approved budget is read-only and cannot be deleted; Variance = Actual − Budget, Variance % = Variance ÷ \|Budget\| (none when the budget is 0), revenue and the net result above budget favourable, expenses above budget unfavourable; "copy actuals" = net posted movement per account (and cost centre when chosen) and month of the previous year × (1 + %), rounded half away from zero; the Excel import replaces every line of a draft (strict); Arabic month names in the Levantine form (كانون الثاني …), the Egyptian form also read; preset roles: Owner/Administrator all three keys, Accountant view + manage, Viewer view, custom roles unchanged (owners add the keys) |

Parties: (a) not kept in a template; (b) kept. Lines: (a) one JSON column like the drafts; (b) a line table | (a) in each case: the parties of control-account lines differ from entry to entry and are chosen when the entry is made (posting still requires them); "description" in the request = the entry description the template fills in; "Save the amounts too" (ticked by default) keeps or drops the amounts; "Use template" replaces the description, Reference and lines on the screen and keeps the date (also `?template=…` from the list); accounts are checked to exist when saved — active / postable / balance / period are checked when the entry is posted; rename = save with a new name (names unique per company); deleting a template does not touch entries made from it; the history shows name, description and Reference changes (the lines column is hidden like a draft's) |
| DT-200 | Accounting dashboard (owner change request 15 Oct 2026, part B, feature 3; **changed by the owner 18 Oct 2026: "Payables" = suppliers, drivers and partners as separate lines with the total, each linking to its accounts page**) | Receivables / payables: (a) open documents as the ageing reports (transport invoices + receivable documents; supplier bills), unapplied receipts shown separately; (b) control-account balances. Budget usage: (a) expenses actual year to date ÷ the year's expense budget (with revenue achieved and the share of the year passed); (b) ÷ the budget to date. "Being entered" for unmapped Supplier deductions: (a) a refused bill with a deduction in the last 30 days (company setting `ACC.SupplierDeductionAttempted`); (b) any supplier bill | (a) in each case. Also: the month is chosen at the top (default the current month; figures as of its end, or today); payables = supplier bills (driver and partner payables have their own screens); reminders: drafts = journal drafts; pending postings unresolved; "previous months still open" = months from the first entry's month to last month that are not closed; overdue = transport invoices (invoice rights) + receivable documents (accounting rights) past due with a balance; bills due = supplier bills with a balance due today … +7 days; rejected e-invoices = rejected or queued with an error (both kinds); budget lines over 100% = expense lines of the approved budget whose actual January → month exceeds their budget for those months (a line without a cost centre takes the actuals of cost centres without their own line); unallocated = active receipts with any amount not allocated. Links open filtered lists: new views "Due within 7 days" (payables) and "Not allocated" (receipts), `?view=` read by the journal list and AP/AR lists. The dashboard opens with any accounting permission (acct.view, je.view/export, acct.post/apar/setup, budget.view, reports.view, settings.manage); each figure and reminder needs the permission of the screen it opens |
| DT-201 | Separate permission "Post journal entries" (owner request 18 Oct 2026, release regression item 6) | Key: (a) new `acc.acct.postentry` (id 26, group Accounting) next to `acc.acct.post`; (b) split `acc.acct.post` into two new keys. | DT-208 | Owner testing 18 Oct 2026 (1): "Add user" showed "Please correct the highlighted fields" with no field highlighted | Cause: the dialog used plain inputs and showed only the problem's `detail` (the generic validation sentence), never its field errors. Fix: (a) the create / invite / set-password dialogs as validated forms (TextField/SelectField + ProblemValidator + ErrorSummary: every server field error on its field with its own message, a refusal without field — e.g. the generic "not available" of DT-203 — spelled out in the summary); client checks before sending (required, username format, email shape, password rules) with the same rules as the server; (b) banner only. Rules: (a) new `GET /companies/{company}/users/password-policy` (users.manage) + `PasswordRules` under the field, shown before typing and ticked while typing; (b) a fixed text. Server message: (a) one code per rule combination (`auth.passwordPolicy`, `…Letter`, `…Digit`, `…Length`) stating "at least N characters, including …"; (b) one vague text. Other screens: (a) the API client turns a validation answer's `detail` into the field messages themselves, so every banner/toast that shows `detail` (no field to highlight) is specific; (b) convert every form | (a) in each case |
| DT-209 | Owner testing 18 Oct 2026 (2): trips list failed for every role ("connection to the server was lost", reference 00000307) | **Cause (a real fault, not the failed build):** the development database had not received the migrations of 18 Oct (`Rev6_PostJournalEntries`, `Rev6_ReceiptNoSearch`); the host neither applied nor checked migrations, so every trips query failed with "Invalid column name 'ReceiptNoSearch'" (500) — reproduced on a clean build of this version. The missing `Rev6_PostJournalEntries` also left the dev database without the posting key (manual entries could not be posted there). **Why the full API run did not catch it:** the API test fixture deletes and recreates its own database and applies every migration at start, so it always runs against the newest schema; nothing ran the code against an older database. Fix of the cause: (a) a start-up version check (`Pipeline/DatabaseVersion.cs`) — Development applies missing migrations itself and logs them, any other environment refuses to start naming them, "Testing" skipped, unreachable database left to the readiness check; (b) only document the update step; (c) apply migrations automatically everywhere. Error screen: (a) the API client remembers each failure's status by its reference and turns "no answer" into status 0; the error box says "connection lost" only for 0, "You don't have permission" for 403 (no retry button), "Something went wrong on the server" + reference otherwise; (b) change every page | (a) in each case — production schema changes stay a deliberate step (idempotent script, Setup_and_Run §3). Dev database updated by the check on 19 Oct 2026 (2 migrations; the 14 existing trips got the search column). Test: `TripListMigrationTests` — trips first, then `Rev6_ReceiptNoSearch` rolled back and applied again, then the list, search and totals for every preset role |
| DT-210 | Owner testing 19 Oct 2026 (1): two-step verification management on the profile | Proof for turn off / change app / new codes: (a) current password AND a current code or an unused recovery code, both required before anything is checked, one answer for any wrong value (`auth.stepUpFailed`, 422 — never 401, which would end the session), failures counted towards the lockout (DT-204); (b) separate answers per field. New app: (a) the new key waits 10 minutes in server memory, bound to the session that started the change and the security stamp, five wrong codes drop it, it replaces the old key only after a code from the new app; (b) replace the key at once (an abandoned change would lock the user out). Turn off: (a) refused while the tenant requires 2FA (`auth.twoFactorRequiredByPolicy`) and always for platform staff (`…RequiredForStaff`); key replaced by a random one and recovery codes deleted; (b) keep them. Sessions: (a) turning on/off, changing the app and the profile password change end every other session at once (also clearing the 30-s cache), this one stays signed in; (b) rely on the stamp (up to 30 s late). Recovery codes: (a) spent with `UPDATE … WHERE UsedAt IS NULL` and a row-count check, called on the store directly (Identity's redeem also saved the user row, which made the one success fail when parallel failures changed that row); (b) a lock. Test helpers (API `Totp.Code`, E2E `totp()`) now hand out a fresh code each time — previous/current/next step, waiting for the next 30-s step when all are used — as a person with an app would | (a) in each case. Consequence: a user who uses a code must wait for the app's next code before using another (e.g. turning 2FA on and signing in again within the same 30 s) |

| DT-189 | Notifications (owner feedback 15 Oct 2026): broken links, panel layout, language, repeated JoFotara credential errors, mark all as read | (a) store type + parameters and resolve text and link when shown; (b) keep fixed text and fix only new links | (a). **Cause of the broken links:** the API returned the company's *name* in the notification's `Company` field and the panel used it as the company part of the address (`/c/<name>/…`); the e-invoice link also named an API path (`/einvoice-submissions/…`) that has no screen. Now `NotificationLinks` resolves every stored link when the list is shown (old ones included): e-invoice → the invoice / credit note / AP-AR document itself (current URL token), pending postings → `accounting/automatic?tab=pending`, export → `exports/<id>` while the file exists, e-invoicing set-up → `settings/e-invoicing`, invitation accepted → `settings/users`, tenant links (`/tenant/…`) only for tenant managers; the company part is the company's URL token. A deleted target gives `LinkProblem` "missing", a screen the reader may not open "forbidden" — the panel shows the reason under the notification instead of opening a broken page. Clicking marks read, closes the panel and opens the screen. Text: `NotificationTexts` (AR/EN title + one sentence per type); messages are stored as `{"p":{…}}` and older `{"Ar","En"}`/plain-text messages are read back into parameters (known wordings) so they are translated too; technical codes (HTTP 401, CORE-401-001, JoFotara reasons) only in `Details` behind a "Details" toggle. Refused credentials (HTTP 401/403, `EInvoiceOutcome.CredentialsRefused`): no per-document notification; one `EINV.SetupError` per company and recipient (submitters + settings managers) linking to e-invoicing settings, the number of affected documents counted when shown (submissions still Rejected with "JoFotara refused the credentials…"), cleared when any document of the company is accepted; older per-document credential notifications are collapsed into it the first time the reader's list is shown (unread if any was unread). Documents refused for credentials stay Rejected as before (resubmit after fixing the settings). Panel: one block per notification (title, message, date) across the panel's width, wrapping long codes, no sideways scroll; "Mark all as read" always shown (disabled when nothing is unread). Imports, report alerts and payments to refund create no notifications today, so they have no link to fix (the resolver passes any other company path through). Simulator: a client id containing "[refuse]" has its credentials refused. |

## Extra indexes / constraints added beyond the design
- `trn.TRN_Trips.ReceiptNoSearch` (persisted computed column, `ReceiptNoKey COLLATE Latin1_General_100_BIN2`) and index `IX_TRN_Trips_ReceiptNoSearch` (CompanyId, ReceiptNoSearch) INCLUDE (Status) WHERE IsDeleted = 0 — owner decision 18 Oct 2026, DT-206, migration `Rev6_ReceiptNoSearch`, revision 6 §B5: performance margin for the "any part of the receipt number" search over 500,000 trips (NFR-01).
- `trn.TRN_Trips` index `IX_TRN_Trips_PartnerOrder` (TenantId, CompanyId, PartnerId, TripDate, ReceiptNoKey, Id) INCLUDE (PartnerCost) WHERE PartnerId IS NOT NULL AND Status = 'Active' AND IsDeleted = 0 — owner findings 27 Oct 2026, DT-271, migration `Rev6_PartnerTripCoverage`, revision 6 §B13: partner trips in payment order for the paid / unpaid rule over 500,000 trips (NFR-01). Views `trn.TRN_PartnerPaymentBoundary` and `trn.TRN_TripPartnerCoverage` (§B13).
- Otherwise none. (The design's indexes only; ISJSON checks per DT-03 are required by the design's JSON convention.)

## Raw SQL used (where EF Core cannot express something)
| File | Why |
|---|---|
| `Hosts/DirectService.Database/Migrations/…_Rev6_ReceiptAllocations.cs` | Revision 6 data step: `INSERT … SELECT` copying every receipt with an `InvoiceId` into `trn.TRN_ReceiptAllocations` (one allocation of its full amount) |
| `database/00_CreateDatabase.sql` | Create the database with collation `Arabic_100_CI_AI_SC_UTF8` and READ_COMMITTED_SNAPSHOT (EF applies the model collation only when it creates the database itself, not in a migration script) |
| `database/90_ApplicationUser.sql` | Application database user `dst_app`: read/write, `DENY DELETE` on core/acc/cfg/trn, `DENY UPDATE` on `core.AuditLogs`, `core.MaintenanceLogs`, `trn.TRN_RateHistory`, `DENY ALTER` on the schemas (verified 8 Oct 2026 on the test database) |
| `tests/…/DatabaseFixture.cs` | `ALTER DATABASE CURRENT SET READ_COMMITTED_SNAPSHOT ON` on the disposable test database |
| `Infrastructure/Persistence/MonthlyRateLock.cs` | `SELECT … FROM trn.TRN_MonthlySiteRates WITH (UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK)` inside the trip transaction (trip save and import), so clearing rates and saving trips serialise (FR-035, B4 review M2) |
| `Infrastructure/Persistence/NumberingService.cs` | `SELECT … FROM cfg.NumberSequences WITH (UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK)` inside the document transaction (BR-038); the increment uses EF `ExecuteUpdate`. `LockAsync` (B2) takes the same lock without issuing a number, so closing a period waits for postings in progress (BR-036) |
| `Infrastructure/Billing/FakePaymentGateway.cs` · `PlatformNumbers` | Platform-wide subscription invoice numbers: `SELECT MAX(...) FROM core.SubscriptionInvoices WITH (UPDLOCK, HOLDLOCK)` inside the invoice transaction (gap-free SUB-) |
| `Infrastructure/Billing/FakePaymentGateway.cs` · `SqlPlatformLock` | `sp_getapplock` / `sp_releaseapplock` (session owner, unpooled connection) so the lifecycle job and demo-company creation run once at a time across instances (DT-136) |
| `Infrastructure/EInvoicing/EInvoiceStore.cs` | ICV: `UPDATE cfg.EInvoiceSettings SET IcvCounter = IcvCounter + 1 OUTPUT inserted.IcvCounter` inside the document's transaction; submission claim `WITH (UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK, READPAST)`; waiting locks `WITH (UPDLOCK, ROWLOCK)` for voids and for storing an outcome |
| `Hosts/DirectService.Database/Migrations/…_Rev6_JournalLineTax.cs` | DT-259: `UPDATE … FROM` copying each entry's Reference to its lines; `INSERT … SELECT … CROSS JOIN (VALUES …)` seeding the nine tax codes for every company; Down guard `IF EXISTS … THROW 50259` |

## Tools (development only)
`tools/design/parse_design.py` (design → model), `checks.py` (check constraints), `gen_efcore.py` (one-off source generation — do not re-run over hand-edited files), `verify_database.py` + `catalog.sql` (verification report), `selftest_verify.py`. `tools/golden-gen` (28 Oct 2026, DT-283): the generator that produced the Architecture.Tests golden files from the restore tag — compiles only against the tag, kept as a record, never run on newer code.

  |---|---|---|---|
  | Microsoft.AspNetCore.SignalR.Common (transitive via SignalR.Client) | 10.0.12 | 10.0.13 | GHSA-334f-vvp4-7g6p (high, DoS) |

  | System.Security.Cryptography.Pkcs (transitive via Microsoft.Data.SqlClient 6.1.6) | 9.0.11 | 10.0.13 (pinned) | GHSA-846f-f764-76mx (high), GHSA-2fmv-rv2c-59x7 (moderate) |
